# Reviews

## Review 1

Very knowledgeable and professional guide! I was late and he was easy going about it. Easy walk around town. Not a huge fan of the market (really smelly), so could have done a flyby there.

## Review 2

What a fabulous walking tour of Stone Town. My guide Ahmad had so much knowledge and stories to share. You simply must take the guided tour to enjoy the full experience and uncover the secrets of Stone Town's past.

## Review 3

We booked the tour last minute however this was not a problem.
Having booked the tour and established where our ships shuttle bus was dropping us off, I messaged our guide.
Ahmad was really quick to respond and happily changed our meeting point to fit in with our shuttle bus drop off point.  It all literally happened within a few minutes.
Ahmad was at our arranged meeting point today,and took us on a great walking tour of Stonetown.  He was incredibly informative and his English was good.  He not only gave us the history of Zanzibar but filled in the gaps in our knowledge of Tanzania.
We spent 3 very hot but really interesting hours winding in and out of the historic town, pausing for photos and generally having a great time.
We would thoroughly recommend Ahmad if youre looking for an informative walking tour of Stonetown.

## Review 4

We had a really great city tour with Ahmed who’s knowledge of Stone Town really brought the place to life and helped us to understand the buildings and architecture alongside the history. Can thoroughly recommend

## Review 5

Booked a walking tour of Stone Town with Ahmed and we both really enjoyed the tour. He paced it according to our interests and gave us a lot of information on the rich history, culture and the current struggles Zanzibarians are facing. We finished off and said goodbye at the Freddy Mercury Museum which we viewed independently.
We originally booked the 1 pm tour but changed it to the 9 am, it was already very hot so we highly recommend the morning if you are there during their hot summer season.

## Review 6

Congratulations for this beautiful visit very rewarding and full of anecdotes. George is a passionate and attentive guide. He was on time and advised us for memories. He waited for us during our shopping and photo breaks.

## Review 11

The dolphin watching was disappointing.  There were no life jackets and the guide in the boat was not very communicative. There were so many boats, and each time the dolphins surfaced, they all took off after them, which ruined the experience. 
We enjoyed seeing the colobus and blue monkeys and the guide from Jozanzi was excellent. 
We were not impressed with Koza cave and felt throughout the tour our driver was not that clear about what would happen at each place and did not engage with us.
We chose to not go to The Rock as we were already over the long distances of driving.

## Review 12

This trip was a great experience. It was great to snorkel with so many different types of fish and see some star fish. Jozani was great and fascinating to see indigenous species; the monkeys were so cute! The cave offered a refreshing dip, followed by a sun soaked one on the beautiful Paje beach. The Rock is well worth a photo pit stop. It was very well organised and my driver couldn''t do enough to make sure I was happy and comfortable. A very highly recommended excursion!

## Review 14

Nous avons réservé cette excursion, et sommes partis en bateau avec deux guides.
Au début tout se passait bien, jusqu’à ce qu’on arrive au repas, où en fait le site vend le lunch, mais ne le paie pas au guide. Du coup celui ci avait un maigre budget pour nous faire manger, essayant de trouver un restaurant pas trop cher et tentant de négocier. Comprenant la chose, cela nous a vite agacé ! Nous passions de restau en restau pour tenter de manger convenablement et tentant de regarder les prix pour rentrer dans le budget ! Du jamais vu ! Un simple repas local sur le bateau nous aurait fait plaisir… Nous avons tenté d’appeler le site, qui ne souhaitait pas élargir le budget pour que l’on mange autre chose qu’une entrée !!! UNE HONTE ! 

PASSEZ VOTRE TOUR C’EST DE L’ARNAQUE !
EXCURSION TRÈS CHER POUR CE QU’ON FAIT !

## Review 16

The prison Island boat ride should have had life jackets. The tour guides were very disorganized and unprofessional. We did not enjoy the stone town because we had been delayed going to prison Island. The spice farm was fantastic. The tour guide at the farm was very knowledgeable and entertaining.

## Review 17

A very immersive tour, through the history, culture, and local economy. At Jambo spice farm we are welcomed with hospitality and generosity; we are guided through the many cultures of local fruits and spices that end, as always, in spice and perfume stalls that the tourist hardly resists without taking something. Lunch is served generously and the flavors of the fruits are delicious. 

Next we go to Prison Island and we have the curiosities of the history, we have the unforgettable experience of interacting with giant turtles, some of them over 100 years old (I was having parties with one that was 158 years old!). The entry to the island is somewhat disorganized and some time was lost in bureaucracy, but everything is forgotten when we take pictures in idyllic settings and interact with almost mythical animals. 

The tour ends with a tour of Stone Town. Once again history makes its presence known, and we are also marked by the brutality of history and the impact of slavery on the Slave Market. It is always worth being confronted with the mistakes of the past so that we don''t repeat them!

## Review 18

The tour description specified private pick up and transfer but did not specifically say if the tour was individual or group. When we found out that the tour was not individual we were surprised and this caused us some troubles. 
The guides in all places were very friendly and helpful. I think it is just needs to be better specified about group trout to set expectations right and manage better the group. If someone wants to do some shopping etc the rest should not wait for 15-20 minutes until they’re done. That was annoying. I would expect the tour guide to manage such moment better

## Review 19

Well organized. On time. Nice lunch. Good guides, well time management. Nice locations seen and flexibility where to go to

## Review 20

Good day. The spice tour was really enjoyable. It was amazing to learn about, smell, and taste so many wonderful spices and fruits strait from their source. It was powerful to learn about and experience the history of Prison Island and the kids loved the tortoises. Stone Town was beautiful and our tour guide Hassan was amazing.

## Review 21

The Spice farm tour was excellent.  Usman the farm guide was a wealth of knowledge with a great sense of humour.  We learnt alot.  We were then transferred back to town for the prison Island and Stone town tour.  Essentially the young guide Hassan was personable enough but basically just escorted us,  he did not really give us much information. he spent a lot of time on his phone. As Australians, tipping is something we don''t do much of and we found that we had to tip Usman, Usman''s assistant, Hassan, and also the driver which makes the trip cost add up.

## Review 22

Fabulous way to end our stay in Zanzibar! The team were amazing throughout and enjoyed drinks and snacks as the sun set - stunning!

## Review 23

We had an incredible experience exploring Stone Town, visiting Jambo Spice Farm, and taking a trip out to Prison Island.
Our main guide was extremely helpful and knowledgeable, bringing the history of Stone Town and Prison Island to life in a really engaging and insightful way. It made the whole experience much more meaningful.
At the spice farm, the guide was absolutely amazing, his depth of knowledge about both traditional and commercial production of fruits and spices in the region was impressive. It was fascinating to see, smell, and taste everything firsthand.
This tour is a must-do for anyone visiting Zanzibar. Highly recommended!
Ps, be ready to tip and have waterproof covers for your phone and wallet if the weather changes.

## Review 24

The tour was very well organized and had no problems. Everything went smoothly.
I would like to especially mention the guides. They are fully involved in their work and make you interested in what they are talking about. They are friendly and polite. Most importantly, they know their subject very well and are happy to share their knowledge.
Unfortunately, I don’t remember the name of the guide at the Spice Farm, but I am very grateful to him for his time and effort.
Mohammed was our second guide. He showed us the island and the city. He is very professional and did a great job. I am very happy we were lucky to meet such a wonderful person.

## Review 27

The tour is split into 3 segments with different guides and drivers so be prepared to tip multiple people if that is your custom.  We really enjoyed all the experiences as each guide was very knowledgeable and personable.  The Spice tour included a lunch of local food sources and was quite tasty.  The history of slavery in Africa, and especially in Zanzibar, is heartbreaking.  Fortunately, Solidarity and Acceptance of differences both racially and spiritually is now the norm, and other countries should learn from this

## Review 28

The tour itself was fantastic and well worth doing! However, I am giving 4 stars instead of 5 due to an issue prior to departure. A few days before the tour, someone from the company messaged me asking to cancel the official booking and pay them directly instead. Doing this would mean losing all booking protection and refund rights if something went wrong, so please be cautious if asked to do this.

As a quick tip for the tour guide: we stopped at a local market to buy fruit to eat on the boat but since I didn''t have an appetite at the time, it went completely untouched. It would be great if leftovers could be packed up so tourist can enjoy them later in the day! Overall, a wonderful tour experience but stick to official booking channels for safety.

## Review 42

O passeio é MARAVILHOSO. Vimos o nascer-do-sol, vimos golfinhos, nadámos com os golfinhos, vimos os corais. A temperatura do mar era muito boa, cederam coletes e óculos.
O povo da Tanzânia é muito simpático e inexcedível na satisfação do turista. Os tanzanianos são sempre atentos ao turista, é um turismo sem qualquer paralelo na Europa. Aqui, em Zanzibar, é tudo bom, começando pelo povo. Obrigado, amigos

## Review 43

Let’s start with this was not a 4 hour tour. For us it lasted 2 hours - including from the time we were picked up at your hotel. We were picked up at 7am and were done by 9:10am. Then - swimming with the dolphins experience went south quickly. Once we navigated to a soo in the ocean we waited for the dolphins. That was ok… however,  at least 10 more boats were already which, at the moment, was not a big deal for us… Once there was word that dolphins had been spotted,   it become,  literally, a boat race to get to the dolphins. All boats circled the dolphins and we were yelled to jump… by the time we and everyone else hit the water, the dolphins had been scared and submerged. We found ourselves in the water getting hit or avoiding being hit by the boats. We were asked to get quickly on the boat so we could chase the dolphins and repeat.  By the second attempt there were just too many boats and we knew it was not safe nor worth to jump in the water at this point. In addition the dolphins were scared so they remain swimming on the bottom of the ocean.  We felt awful for the dolphins! 
After this we went snorkeling. The experience was good but again too many boats and people.  We snorkeled for about 15 minutes.  When we returned to the boat, we were offered pineapple, papaya, ripe avocado, and small bananas which were just cut without any sanitary consideration, while we were swimming and placed in a tray on the floor of the boat.  So the tray got salt water on it almost immediately. This is raw-No napkins, no plates, no sanitary protocol. 
NO SANDBAR - part of the tour included the sandbar.  we were told there was no sandbar because at that time of the day the water is high. Wish they have clarified this in their promotion.  So we headed back to the shore where we were dropped at a beach to get transported back to our hotel - under other circumstances I would have come to terms and accepted the disappointing experience  but with a price tag of $135 per person - it is a rip off.

## Review 101

Our safari was canceled two separate times : November 30th and December 2nd. After the second time, we requested a refund.

## Review 102

Amazing!  If this is your first time in East Africa, this is a must-do. It is easily one of the highlights of my time in Zanzibar. I was a bit nervous, but the company confirmed the booking via WhatsApp, the airport pickup was prompt, and the safari itself was unbelievable. 

I didn’t know what to anticipate, so I was surprised that the plane landed us in the middle of the park. There is no airport to speak of, but I immediately saw an elephant!  

Tips:

(1) If you are tall, be sure to be one of the first people on the plane by lining up right away. You’ll want an exit seat and there is no assigned seating. Otherwise you’ll be very uncomfortable in the right seating. 

(2) The plane ride is only 45 minutes, but it can be very hot. You won’t regret a personal fan. 

(3) You’ll be hungry because chances are you won’t eat breakfast before the 6am pickup. Lunch isn’t until 12:30. Plan ahead and you won’t be stuck with the one (terrible) cafe at the airport. A sandwich and chips would have been clutch. Water was provided, but if you freeze a bottle of water the night before you’ll be happy you did. 

(4) The provided lunch is buffet style and we found it quite good.  I did fine as a vegetarian. 

(5) This is the time for a good camera if you’re in the market for one. Be sure to bring a backup battery for your cell phone if you’re using it for photos because you’ll be on it nonstop all day. 

Enjoy!

On the 7th I didn’t receive any information on wether we were going on the tour the next day. I had to text the people late afternoon only to get a reply at 7pm after I texted, the second time, asking for my money back if the tour was not going ahead.  The whole thing was such a huge inconvenience as we had to change our holiday plans to accommodate the new date and the guys didn’t even apologise or acknowledge the inconvenience this whole thing caused. I wish I could give zero stars because I got nothing but a massive headache from these guys and how unprofessional they are.

I would say stay away from these guys as there’s no guarantee you’ll actually go on the tour on the dates you booked and the customer service is horrible.

## Review 106

10/10 — Highly recommend! This excursion was even better than we expected. Although it was about a two-hour drive from the northwest coast, it was a great way to explore southeastern Zanzibar. We loved that it was a private tour, which gave us plenty of time to enjoy each stop at our own pace. The fresh seafood lunch was delicious, the cave was peaceful and beautiful, and ending the day at a beach party with music, drinks, a bonfire, and a stunning sunset was the perfect finish. If you’re looking for a more relaxed day of exploring Zanzibar, this excursion is definitely worth it!

## Review 107

It’s a nice experience to see different parts of Zanzibar. So I would recommend it however, the tour guide may not be as enthusiastic as you would expect a tour guide to be. And several stuff were made without clear communication on why those staff were made with other people picked up, but I don’t know if they’re part two or not or if they were just family members as communication was very limited.

## Review 108

Amazing and beautiful place. Perfect guide. 100% recommendations. And sure im going to come back, that’s the paradise 🌴😎🌴

## Review 109

This was truly an amazing experience — the perfect way to start my vacation in Zanzibar! I was able to see and do so much in just one day. It genuinely felt like getting a full “taste” of Zanzibar: activities, culture, key landmarks, and an authentic local feel all wrapped into one unforgettable excursion.

My tour guide was one of the most communicative and attentive people I’ve ever worked with. He checked in the day before to confirm everything, reached out again the morning of, and kept me updated the entire time. As a solo traveler in a foreign country, that level of communication and reassurance meant everything.

The day flowed beautifully. We started with visiting the turtles while he shared incredible history during the drive. I even got to play with a little monkey and take some amazing photos. Then we headed to the beach, where I met a local woman who made the most creative coconut crafts — such a special touch. After a delicious late lunch, we explored a serene cave where I was able to relax, recharge, and truly feel the healing energy of the space. We wrapped up the evening with a vibrant beach party that felt like the perfect finale.

This tour gave me adventure, culture, relaxation, nature, and nightlife all in one. I can’t wait to do it again. If you’re considering it… book it!

## Review 110

If you’re wanting to explore the island and see many places in one day, then this is the tour for you! Said, our tour guide and driver, was great! All of the places included are definitely worth a visit.

## Review 111

+great customer service (Amina was our guide)
-you’ll want to plan to tip several different people (guide, cooking class, coconut drink, driver) so have a lot of change in cash or shillings as they do not take cards for that or purchases.  It’s definitely deserving but I’d like to just tip one person then have them divide it out. 
-cooking class is not like shown in the picture for this excursion, you will be cooking on the ground.
+the food was great from the cooking class experience 
-you didn’t have to pay to use bathrooms but they aren’t in the best condition

100% recommend. Guide was on time very personable. Drive down the coast through the villages was very nice. Food at Usumba Rock was very delicious. ## Review 114

The best way to experience Zanzibar’s natural and cultural wonders.   It is also a great way to spend a full day away from your Stone Town or Beach Hotel to see the southern part of the island.    Said will show you a great time along with conversations of anything you want to know about Zanzibar.   It’s a packed day of fun, food and memories that you will take with you when you leave Zanzibar.   Lots of picture taking opportunities ending the day with the eastern sunset, music, acrobatics and bonfire.   You will end the day riding a boat from the beach to Said’s van to take you home.  It’s a fuil day of activities to experience as a part of this private tour.  We had a great time and glad we invested in this trip!  Well worth it!.

There is no pick up as stated. We were walked around areas of stone town which we had visited several times during our visit for 40 mins. The guide had little communication with us. Unser Guide Amine war jedoch sehr gut und hat sich sehr um uns bemüht.

## Review 137

Our friendly driver Salim collected us from the hotel and drove us to Stone Town where we were met by our tour guide, Hassan. He walked us around the historic parts of the city with lots of information and checking we understood, answering any questions. His English was very good. 

We weren’t entirely convinced we got the best prices at the gift shop and spice stall he encouraged us to stop at but we were also happy to invest in the local economy. 

The slavery exhibition was excellent at telling the story of this brutal part of history, and the memory of standing where the slaves were held before market will stay with us for a long time. 

Then to be taken to the Spice Farm and eat an absolutely delicious home cooked meal with stunning views was a joy. 

The Spice Farm tour was informative and entertaining, again our guide Yazan had excellent English and we enjoyed learning about the different spices and plants grown there. 

The journey was safe and we’d recommend this to any traveller

## Review 138

We had such a wonderful time on our **Stone Town and spice farm tour** today! Yasser, our guide, was absolutely brilliant. He did an excellent job of sharing the fascinating history of Stone Town with us.

What made it truly special was how incredibly patient and understanding Yasser was with our elderly parents. He walked at a comfortable, leisurely pace through the charming streets, making sure everyone felt at ease. It was a truly **great experience** for the whole family!

## Review 139

This was a wonderful experience. Walking through Stone Town and listening to the history as well as testing our knowledge of the spices on the spice farm was amazing. Yasser was an excellent tour guide, frequently checking to see if we had any questions and making sure the experience was more than what we had expected. He was responsive and was even helpful in offering suggestions for other tours that made sense for our itinerary and where we were staying. All the drivers provided to and from were prompt and friendly. We were truly able to have a great experience with no worries or as is said, Hakuna Matata. I will be reaching out to Yasser on any future trips to Zanzibar and recommend this tour as well as any others Yazan tours has to offer.

## Review 140

El tour de las especias estuvo genial, el chico estaba entusiasmado y nos explicaba todo con muchos detalles, la comida estaba muy buena, nos dijo que la cocinaban las mujeres de allí, sin embargo por la tarde al visitar stone town se veía que estaba cansado y casi ya no nos explicaba nada, también al preguntarle no sabia muy bién que responder porque no estaba bién informado, en la iglesia no nos explico nada, simplemente se sentó 5 minutos y nos fuimos, el recorrido fue muy corto por stone town viendo todo muy ràpido y sin explicar nada. Nos quedamos con ganas de ver más en stone town. Te obligan a dar propina por todo, el hombre que sube a la palmera, al que te hace unos sombreros con palma, el que te da fruta…

## Review 141

Jozani forest trip is soothing, interesting and funny due to Monkeys and nature. Mangrove forest is surprising and interesting, nice exploration, good guide for both. 
Spice farm is the best part, staff is funny, welcoming and deserves all the tips and in-site purchases due to their hard and interesting work. You’ll be happy to support the village and meet all the local people (if you can, bring some gifts for the children like pencils and sweets). Overall, Spice Farm is a must-go for a Zanzibar travel! 
Stone Town tour is a bit disappointing: very crowded town, feeling of being aimed constantly from vendors doesn’t allow you to look a lot around you in a calm way, if you’re a Woman you’ll be object of uncomfortably staring and comments from the local people (especially in the fish market) and honestly that could ruin your trip. A city to visit if you can place layers and filters in your judgment and just live the pulsating heart of Zanzibar, we strongly recommend to ask and talk to guide to meet your requirement (eating, bathroom, suggestions) and you’ll be satisfied with almost no issues. However, our guide’s explanation wasn’t always very clear nor involving and sometimes dropped sexist jokes which weren’t funny. Driver was not very talkative but overall ok.

## Review 142

We had a fabulous time . Driver took great care of us and we had brilliant guides at all of our stops. They all had fantastic knowledge and showed us all the highlights. We loved the Jozani forest where we found out about the various trees and we took some great pictures of the monkeys. At the Spice Farm we were shown where all the spices came from and what they looked like on the tree. Smelt nutmeg, lemongrass , turmeric, cloves etc and got a chance to buy some of them. Also had a fruit tasting session. At the end we were presented with woven headgear etc. Guides were friendly and asked what we wanted to experience. As part of the trip we visited a small African project and got to hold various chameleon s. 
At Stone Town we had anther fabulous guide whose knowledge was outstanding. He took us all over the town where we saw historical sites, and the markets . A memorable day packed with out standing sites which were enhanced by the superb guides and of course our driver. Big thanks to them all . We have some amazing pictures.

We made a new friend today - his name is Masoud

We booked this full-day tour to see a bit of everything, and it didn''t disappoint. The dolphin watching portion was exciting, and we managed to get some great photos. Nungwi and Kendwa beaches are stunning—the white sand and turquoise water look like a postcard. Maldives whooo?

Amina (the only female guide in her company) was our guide. She was well informed and very knowledgeable. At the beginning she asked what our interests are. We said history and culture. She proceeded to give the normal tour but expanded it with the interesting history and the historical and current culture of Zanzibar. It was the first day of Ramadan and she explained many of the rules & customs around Ramadan. If we return to Zanzibar we will definitely request Amina to be our guide

## Review 189

This private tour included a visit to the Tangawizi spice farm followed by a walking tour of Stone Town.

The Tangawizi “spice farm” was not really a farm in the traditional sense. It was a couple of acres on the outskirts of Stone Town with various plants growing there. That said, we did see examples of nutmeg, vanilla, cocoa, coffee, cinnamon, jackfruit, ginger, turmeric, and lemongrass. It was informative at a basic level, but if you are looking for a true working spice farm experience, I would recommend seeking out a larger, more authentic operation.

After the spice stop, we continued with a walking tour of Stone Town. The route through the vegetable, fish, meat, and spice markets was visually fascinating and lively. Unfortunately, the guide’s explanations were fairly perfunctory. He spent more time greeting friends and acquaintances than explaining the history, culture, or nuances of what we were seeing.

Our guide (Mr. P) was knowledgeable but not very enthusiastic. He had difficulty making eye contact and made little effort to engage with us personally or to learn anything about us, which made the experience feel transactional rather than “private.”

Several practical aspects could have been improved, especially given the heat. Water was not offered (we had to ask), restroom breaks were not proactively suggested, and there were no suggestions to rest during the walking portions.

The van itself was adequate and nicely air-conditioned. The driver’s English was limited, but he eagerly—perhaps a bit too eagerly—engaged us about an upcoming football match. Once football topics were exhausted (and so was his English), he turned on music.

We met the guide at the spice farm, and he returned with us to Stone Town. During the drive, the guide and driver spoke to each other and made no effort to include us. They asked to play “Swahili music,” which turned out to be about 30 minutes of techno rap in Swahili accompanied by videos of gangsters throwing money around. One song featured women singing “who the f*** is the master?” This felt like a poor and somewhat jarring representation of Zanzibar and Tanzania.

Overall, the tour covered interesting places, but the delivery, engagement, and attention to guest comfort significantly detracted from the experience. With more thoughtful guiding and better logistics, this could be much stronger.

## Review 193

Fantastic day picked up by Ibrahim and Ali who made our day special, safe and informative, I''m not a great come and buy some products guy but the Spice Farm was very informative and the Prison  Island Tour was made by the zaniest boat trip ever where we virtually bounced  off four walls.. ....awesome!!! Saw lots of old Tortoises 🐢 😕 ha ha  and then walked around a mainly inaccessible Stone Town where we had hoped to visit the old Fort...shut and Museum shut etc but we walked around and soakedup the atmosphere in a very very safe environment. ## Review 194

This tour is not wheelchair accessible in any reasonable sense of the phrase, though the guide did his best and got us to all the listed stops. My wife was lifted, in her wheelchair, into and out of the boat by four men. I feared for both my wife and the chair!

Prison Island was possibly not worth such an effort, but that’s only our opinion. The tortoises were interesting and plentiful.

Stone Town was good, though the limitations of wheelchair accessibility were obvious in that internal visits were off the agenda. We had realised this beforehand, as it’s almost impossible to make a historic place like this accessible.

Our favourite stop was the spice farm. We both enjoyed the fruit tasting and being shown the various spice plants and trees. The paths around the various plantings are just about accessible with the chair, though there are multiple roots to be negotiated.

As other reviewers have said, there are various people other than the main guide who expect tips along the way, so have some small dollar bills to hand.

Overall it was enjoyable, but I believe we were lucky to have a guide who made it work.

## Review 195

This tour was advertised as wheelchair accessible with flat surfaces, which is completely inaccurate.

While we genuinely enjoyed the Spice Farm, describing it as accessible is a stretch. My husband uses an electric wheelchair and another passenger had significant mobility issues. We clearly advised the operator of these needs prior to the tour, yet the experience was anything but smooth. Our guide was friendly and helpful at the Spice Farm, however navigating the site was very difficult for both my husband and the other passenger. Thankfully there were occasional places to sit and rest.

We were also told to tip two workers USD $5 each, which we did, but this was an unexpected additional cost on an already expensive tour.

Before heading to Stone Town, we specifically checked with our guide, Ali, about Prison Island and Stone Town accessibility. He advised Prison Island was not accessible due to many steps, meaning two members of our group would not be able to participate. We asked if an alternative could be arranged — he repeatedly said he would check, but never did.

By the time we arrived in Stone Town and walked to the Slave Museum, it had already been a long walk for our less mobile passenger. We then asked how far it was to walk back to the port through Stone Town and were told 30 minutes, despite being told it was 5 minutes just moments earlier. There was absolutely no way our friend could manage that distance.

Unfortunately, the driver had already been dismissed by the guide. We had to insist he be called back so we could return to the ship. At that point, we were done with a tour that let us down. 

To say we are disappointed is an understatement. I contacted the operator to express our concerns and have received no response. We have requested a 50% refund directly from the operator and have now escalated the matter through Viator. In all my years of travelling, I have never felt the need to request a refund — until now.

Tip for operators: Do not advertise tours as wheelchair accessible or suitable flat surfaces when they are not. People with disabilities rely on honesty when booking experiences. What makes this worse is that our accessibility needs were communicated in advance and still could not be met.

No offer to refund the Prison Island Tour!

## Review 206

The tour itself didn’t really align with the description. There was no snorkeling and the guide didn’t have the same itinerary from the company that was on Viator. In Stone Town he asked what we wanted to see and didn’t have a prepared tour. We also had to share the itinerary with him to be able to make the stops at the church and old dispensary. He wasn’t aware those were supposed to be stops. 

Also, it would have been good to know that at each stop, a local guide would take you instead of the main guide you’re with. Each of these local guides also expect a tip, so we ended up having to tip 4-5 additional people we didn’t expect to.

A set lunch time also wasn’t part of the full day tour. We knew it wasn’t included, but assumed there would be a set time to stop for an opportunity to get lunch. We ended up not eating until almost the end of the tour when we had to ask to be taken somewhere. 

Overall, the tour was informative and we did see all the main attractions mentioned. It just wasn’t the smoothest and not quite as expected.

Leider hatten wir wohl falsch verstanden, dass unter Guide verstanden wird. Ibrahim war mehr ein Begleiter, er hat uns von Punkt zu Punkt gebracht. Er hat jedoch kaum etwas erklärt über die Insel/Kultur oder den Ausflug. Wir sind eifach pberall durchgelaufen ohne Erklärung. Sehr schade.

## Review 242

We were picked up at 8:30 am by our guide and his driver Simba.

The spice farm was great, our guide seemed to be energetic, knowledgeable and welcoming. We enjoyed walking around and seeing everything from the source and learning what everything was used for. We watched people climbing trees to fetch coconuts and we were given crowns made out of leaves. 

Please be aware, you are taking to product tables and expected to purchase from them at the end. 

Next was prison island, this is where our guide seemed to lose his enthusiasm. We got on a boat to get there which was fun and when we arrived he just kind of left us to it and said go and have a look and find me when you''ve finished. He then showed us where the tortoises were, and said the same again ''you have a look and I''ll wait here for you''. Pretty disheartening when all the other tourists were getting shown around and given information as they walked around. 

Lastly we went to stone town. When we arrived he just said, ''what do you want to see?'' We stated that we didn''t know what was here and this is why we booked a tour guide. He just walked us around not really saying much and asked if we could leave multiple times. We said we wanted to go to a rooftop bar, which we did and it was incredible. But we wouldn have done if we didn''t mention it. 

When we got back to the car, the driver had parked somewhere he shouldn''t of. So he got clamped and didn''t have enough money to pay the fine. So, we just sat and waited for around an our until they managed to find the money. 

All in all, it was an okay day. But if you do decide to go on this tour, I suggest you research stone town and decide what you want to see before you get there. And I''d do your research on the history of each place, because you don''t really get much info on the tour. 

The guide and the driver were nice enough guys, but it just seemed a bit half hearted and unorganised.

## Review 319

Wie der Titel es sagt - fünf Sterne sind viel zu wenig. Jungs und sein Fahrer haben uns einen unfassbaren Ausflug beschert.
Zunächst mal gingen wir davon aus in Einer Gruppe zu fahren - nein der ganze Trip war nur alleine ! Das erste Highlight war auf persönlichen Wunsch der Halt in einer lokalen Schule !
Die Gewürzfarm war ein weiteres Highlight - ein unfassbar Fülle an Gewürzen und Früchten welche auch alle probiert werden konnten .
Die Gefängnisinsel mit den bis zu 198 Jahre alten Schildkröten und die anschließende Führung in Stone Town waren ebenso überwältigend !
Auf gutem deutsch und auch mit Erklärungen zu den dunkleren Zeiten der Sklaverei und einer offen und freundlichen Art fühlten wir uns mehr als wohl .
Selbst als wir durch vier oder fünf Geschäfte streifen wollten um einheimische Kleidung zu kaufen war Juma stets ohne Stress und Eile.
Und krönender Abschluss war die Rückfahrt mit deutschem Schlager und gemeinsamen Gesang 😅.
Vielen Dank für diesen Ausflug den wir niemals vergessen werden ! Nur zu empfehlen !

## Review 320

An amazing day that started at the Spice Farm which was a highlight during our time on Zanzibar. The guides and explanations were exemplary with a chance to smell and taste a lot of the produce from the farm.

The trip to Prison Island was fun, a short trip on a boat to see the giant tortoises is well worth it and there''s a great photo spot overlooking the water for couples!

Finally the guided walk around Stone Town was interesting, a town steeped in history that was explained in detail by our guide. Fully recommend this day out!

## Review 330

Safari part was good. The booking agent and logistics getting there and getting back not so much.

## Review 331

Even though we went on a safari in Mikumi National Park at the end of November, during the small rainy season, we were more than thrilled and saw a lot of wildlife. We spotted 4 of the Big 5, though unfortunately there are no more rhinos in the park.

During the day, we were even lucky enough to see a leopard, which our guide said is very rare. It was truly a wonderful day, and our guide was simply fantastic! While other jeeps drove past certain animals, we stopped for each and every one, giving us plenty of time to take photos and enjoy the experience.

Since it was the low season, there were also far fewer jeeps than what is often shown online, which made the safari even more enjoyable.

I specifically chose this tour because it was advertised as being offered in German. Despite several German-speaking tourists being on the tour, there was no German-speaking guide. I chose this tour because my partner doesn’t speak English well, and it would have been more comfortable for him. I don’t understand why it’s advertised this way if it’s not actually available.

Despite that, it was still a beautiful and exciting day, and we thoroughly enjoyed the experience.

## Review 332

Such an amazing experience. Unforgettable moment can be able to see all those animals in Africa 🦒🦓🐘🦍🦌

Si potrebbe gestire meglio la visita alle tartarughe perché il posto è piccolino e 4 ore sono troppe da passare lì... magari aggiungendo nel prezzo la visita allo zoo lì vicino.

## Review 341

Para empezar nuestro guía Nasser fué fantástico! Sabe hablar español, fué muy amable con nosotros. La recogida puntual con coche privado y aire acondicionado. Nos fuimos a ver delfines, lástima que hubiera más de 20 barcas detrás de los delfines y por miedo a que nos pasara una barca por encima no nos tiramos a verlos en el agua, en algún momento parecía como si estuvieramos haciendo carreras… nos dejaron cerca de la isla mnemba para hacer esnorkel y ver peces mientras nos prepararon una fantástica bandeja de fruta con sandía, piña, naranja, fruta de la pasión, aguacate y plátanos. Luego nos llevaron al aquarium de baraka donde tuvimos mucho tiempo para nadar con las tortugas y hacernos muchas fotos. Nos dejaron tiempo para comer en un restaurante de Nungwi y luego nos llevaron a hacer un crucero en dhow, la única pega es que el barco estaba demasiado lleno y casi no podías moverte, tenías que estar con las piernas dobladas… por lo demás fué uno de los mejores días que tuvimos en Zanzíbar.

## Review 342

10 of us U.S. journalists and professional friends traveled together. The informed and amiable tour guide taught us a lot about how spices are grown on the island. The lunch they cooked for us at the end was, we agreed, one of the best meals we had in Zanzibar - and all were good. Plus, all of us bought some of the great soap, spice, coffee, and other products before leaving. Highly recommend this!

I was driven to the farm. I had a most intensive tour of all the spice plants and was dressed up in garlands of palm leaves and hibiscus! Everything was good and the guide was happy to let me sit and rest as I needed.

## Review 346

Seeing the dolphins was great in their natural habitat, however there were so many boats, that most probably scared them, so this does need to be looked at. 
The island we were supposed to visit was private my assumption was that we could snorkel off of it, but this was not the case, so slightly misleading however seeing all the fish in the sea was amazing. 
The beaches we were taken too were beautiful and well worth visiting, the only downside is the continuous hassle you get from beach vendors it does not stop!

## Review 347

Caveat Emptor! Although they use a sailing ship to sell you on this tour there is no sailing on this tour. Although they call it a Mnemba Island tour there is no going to Mnemba Island on this tour. you board an old fragile fiberglass dinghy to chase down dolphins, then use a rusty old ladder to get in and out of the water, A tetanus shot waiting to happen. You maybe get less than a minute of time in the water before the dolphins take off or the other 10 boats of people scare them off. its not worth the price. Especially when they misrepresent what actually happens. Feeding sea turtles at the aquarium was the only highlight.

Best trip I’ve done in Zanzibar. Simba was a great driver and Amina was an incredible guide. She was very knowledgeable, very friendly and was always making sure I was having a good time and getting what I wanted out of the trip. I couldn’t recommend the pair of them enough. Pick up and drop off were exactly the times originally stated which was a pleasant surprise based on other trips too 10/10

## Review 350

## Review 351

BRILLIANT!! 

I got to see dolphins and snorkel and the tour was amazing. So informative and well planned. It was loads of fun, and the time we had with people was great!

## Review 352

Belle journée avec Ibrahim et Simba qui ont parfaitement su s’adapter a ma demande et respecter mon rythme. L’aquarium de tortues est un petit bassin d’eau salé qui ne vaut pas forcément le détour . La plage de Mnemba est la plus belle et agreable et la plus authentique des 3 plages. Beaucoup de sollicitations par des vendeurs de plage partout, difficile de passer un moment seule 
Nungwi est bordee d’hotels mais le site reste beau pour kendwa , immeubles kitesurf et beaucoup de vents zone touristique pas nécessaire et manquant d’authenticité 
Mnemba vaut le detour .

## Review 353

I wish I would have spent the day back at my hotel by the beach. The only reason I’m giving this review a 2 star is because of the lovely time we had with the turtles and beautiful pictures that were taken for us by the workers at the aquarium. 
Our tour guide was very basic, at times I thought she had no clue what she was doing. She turned up nice and early at our hotel but just sat at reception, didn’t notify anyone just sat there until I came looking for her. 
Long story short, don’t bother with the dolphin swimming. Such a waste of time. 30+ boats all chasing 1 dolphin. Tour guide had no clue and even tried to get us on the boat without fins until we kicked up a fuss. She said her captain only borrows fins from one supplier and he had run out. I had to force her to use another supplier. 
Once on the boat her instructions where ‘when I say jump you jump ok’ as soon as a dolphin was seen she would shout ‘JUMP, look down look down’ an absolute joke. 
The visits to the beach were even worse. We got the entrance of Nungwi beach and she said what do you think of the beach. We replied nice and she said ok let’s go. 
Then we went to Kenwa beach and same thing. 
Simply wasted our day to drive from the south of the island all the way to the north for this. 
If you want to do real snorkelling use Safari blue.

## Review 354

The tour is fine and the tour guide was friendly. 
The turtle zoo could be skipped, too many people and facility in bad condition.

## Review 355

This tour was truly a memorable one. It was really fun swimming with the family of dolphins, snorkeling with many colorful fish, feeding the sea turtles and then relaxing at all the beaches. This was a great tour and highly recommend!

## Review 356

The day excursion was great and filled with many things to do. I wish there was more explanation of what was going to take place initially. We were driven to the north side of the island and then were able to go to the destinations listed. In my mind we were going to take a boat from stone town to the north side of the island. But again, it was fun and we were able to see lots of different things. A variety of fruits were provided.

## Review 358

Was a beautiful trip around the mangrove the kids loved it. Really good guide
Recommend checking time to go due to tide level.

## Review 359

Marshed was a very good tour guide through the mangroves. We had to paddle hard for a bit against the current to get to them but then it was easy. Just the two of us so it was very peaceful. I am a fairly strong kayaker and I think that got us through the available pathways perhaps quicker than normal, so we just relaxed and floated for a bit at the end. I very much appreciate it the mango and coconut milk at the end. I would recommend this experience.

## Review 360

An Absolute 5-Star Experience with Ramjun!
I had a fantastic time on this tour! Ramjun reached out to us on WhatsApp the day beforehand to ensure we were ready and set for the tour the next day.

When Ramjun provided a brief of the itinerary, we asked if we could change it up slightly and skip the Nakupenda Beach Nature Reserve portion since we had already done that tour previously. Because we were quite tired from previous tours, we also asked if we could make the rest of the tour a bit shorter. Ramjun was completely accommodating and flexible with our request!

He speaks really good English and is extremely knowledgeable about history, you can truly tell how much he loves it. He even checks in to see whether you prefer long or short historical details while on the tour, easily tailoring the experience to your liking.

He was professional from start to finish, and I would highly recommend this tour to anyone visiting Zanzibar!

He was incredibly knowledgeable, friendly, and passionate about sharing the history and culture of Zanzibar. He answered all of our questions, made us feel welcome throughout the trip, and ensured everything ran smoothly.

What we especially appreciated was that Decko also gave us honest local advice on the best places to buy souvenirs and genuine Tanzanite, helping us avoid overpriced tourist traps. ## Review 389

The trip began with the guide Khalid and driver picking us up at 0830 before travelling to a port just outside Stone Town where we boarded a boat with another couple and headed to Prison Island. Khalid gave us a wonderful tour of the island with lots of information and time to read and explore the little museum. We then took the boat to Nakupenda where the tide was out and we had time to swim, snorkel (though there wasn’t much to see) and sunbath whilst Khalid expertly prepared a seafood lunch with his team. The final and best stop was Stone Town, where Khalid gave us a walking tour of the town by foot, pointing out historical and architectural insights, a spot of shopping and a ‘Welcome to Zanzibar’ video opportunity with the locals at the port. :) The most harrowing part of the tour was sitting in the dungeon where slaves were kept… a very emotional moment. After visiting the market, Khalid and his driver returned us to our hotel having had an interesting cultured day exploring Zanzibar.

We insisted on being returned to our hotel and asked for a refund. Later, the tour company issued a partial refund. I find this unacceptable. They knew it was raining and were purposely deceptive. ## Review 407

I did this tour as a solo traveller and had a brilliant experience with my guide, Mohamed. He was extremely polite, very knowledgeable, and gave great information throughout the visit to Prison Island.

The tour starts with a speedboat ride to the island, which is home to a tortoise reserve as well as peacocks, peahens, and chicks. The tortoises were incredible — the oldest is 200 years old! Mohamed shared lots of interesting facts about the wildlife, including the tortoises, the deer, and even the fancy pigeons (definitely not your typical British pigeons!).

After spending time with the tortoises, we walked around to the main prison complex. There’s a café/bar, some lovely gift shops, and a beautiful panoramic view across the sea. There’s also a small museum with helpful information about the building’s history.

Next, we headed to the snorkelling spot. I was under time pressure to get to another tour, and there was a bit of rain, so I didn’t end up snorkelling — but the water was crystal clear and full of visible sea life, including starfish, fish, sea urchins, and more. We were also given cassava chips as a snack, which were great.

A practical note: if you plan to snorkel, you usually arrive in your swimwear as there isn’t a clean changing area on the island. Most people head back to their hotel to change afterwards. Since I had a walking tour immediately after, it didn’t work out for me this time. On a hot day you might dry quickly, or you could bring spare clothes and use a restroom if needed.

Overall, this was a fantastic tour — beautiful scenery, a very knowledgeable guide, and Mohamed is also highly skilled at taking great photos!

For solo travellers: the website says it’s suitable for individuals, which it absolutely is. The team were very accommodating, but there weren’t others in the group on this occasion, so it ended up being a private tour. This made it more flexible for me, though it does mean you may or may not meet other travellers.

Highly recommended

We were so looking forward to this tour experience today. However, it was worse than what any paying customer should ever have to endure.

ASIDE from the fact that the boat engine broke down leaving from Prison Island to Nakupenda Sand Bar, and a 10 minute trip took almost 90 minutes to reach the sand bar...hakuna matata at least we made it. Then they started setting up the lunch tent...and when we saw the disrepair of their tent (torn, with holes, and filthy) and the table we were supposed to enjoy lunch on appeared came from a local junkyard, we respectfully told our guide we wanted to return back immediately as at this point we did not trust the quality of the food we were going to be served. Look at the pics...I''m sure any paying customer would agree.

However, they ignored our repeated requests to leave and kept setting up the tent and lighting the charcoals as if we were going to stay and eat under the shambles of their setup.

Now we know this is Africa, but every other tent set up around us was beautiful.  We looked like we payed for the no cost tour. But this tour cost us over $250!!!!  They insisted on continuing setting up until we start enchanting no no no -- take us back. And all during this time they were mocking the situation and laughing like little children. THEN they insisted on taking us back -- on the broken boat 😬🤐😠😧. We said absolutely no, ESPECIALLY SINCE SAFETY WAS AT STAKE.

Finally a fellow boat operator approached us and offered to lend them their boat to bring is back to Stone Town...and it took less than 10 minutes to arrive back!!

When we departed the boat, we told our guide that we cannot continue the tour as she completely disrespected us...and without apologizing, she just walked away, no apology.

Now we are sitting at Cape Town Fish Market about to enjoy a wonderful lunch on our own at 3 pm, the same time our tour was supposed to continue until 5 pm. 

We will enjoy the rest of our day exploring safe Stone Town alone, and will find out own transportation back to our hotel.  We will not mention the tour guides name out of respect, but this Tour Agency should not exist given the extremely nonexistent customer service they offered.  STAY AWAY from MANGO TOURS at all cost.

P.S. when we were stranded on the water heading to the sand bar from Prison Island, they tried to reach the tour agency with no avail. No one answered!  Good thing out boat was not capsized!!

P.P.S.  we never had a bad but experience on the many tours we have taken through Viator and expect we will be refunded for this terrible experience.  Hopefully no one will have to endure such an experience like we had today.

It was a perfect day trip for a family. Lots of stunning views, great guide, and a relaxed trip with no rush and time to explore on our own. Overall great value for the price!

We returned on land to Stone Town where we saw Freddy Mercury''s house and visited the central bazaar where we had time for shopping. We were shown the constructive styles of the houses from the perspective of the civilizations that left their mark on the place (Arabs and Indians).
The walk ended in the city''s main square, where we were picked up by car to the hotel.
Recommended 100%.

Nicolae Daniel Radu & Eduard Petre from Bucharest-Romania.

## Review 484

During my recent trip to Zanzibar organized by this company, I had a mixed experience that fell short of expectations.

The tour took place during heavy rainfall, and unfortunately, there was a lack of clear communication regarding the weather conditions and the option to reschedule or cancel due to safety concerns. The decision to proceed with the tour despite the heavy rain made it challenging and unsafe, causing discomfort as we were not adequately prepared for such weather conditions.

The tour guide, albeit friendly and helpful, was unequipped to handle the rain, leaving us waiting for a significant amount of time under inadequate shelter until the rain subsided. This delay impacted the overall experience, limiting our ability to enjoy and explore as anticipated. The visit to the Spicy Market also fell short, as I encountered unexpectedly inflated charges, seemingly influenced by connections of the tour guide.

There were positive aspects, such as the delicious food provided and the commendable English-speaking abilities of the team. However, these were overshadowed by the adverse weather conditions and certain disappointing aspects of the tour, leading to a less than satisfactory experience.

Although the team''s effort and the guide''s language skills were appreciated, the overall tour failed to meet my expectations due to safety concerns, unexpected additional charges, and the inability to enjoy the tour due to the heavy rain. There''s room for improvement in ensuring better communication and preparedness in adverse weather conditions.

I hope that these points are taken constructively to enhance the overall experience for future travelers.

## Review 499

Nous avons réservé un séjour safari au parc de Mikumi sur 3 jours au départ de Zanzibar avec Alfa tour et Safari. J''ai été vraiment très surprise de l''organistation de très haute qualité. Alfa est venu nous chercher à l''hôtel en voiture, ensuite nous avons pris le ferry au départ de Stone Town pour Dar El Salem et ensuite le train jusqu''à Morogoro puis la voiture jusqu''à Mikumi. Le voyage c''est tres bien passé et était très bien organisé. Notre guide était fabuleux et très attentionné, il faisait tout pour nous rendre le séjour agréable. Ensuite nous avons visité la parc de Mikumi, c''était magique 🤩, nous avons vu de nombreux animaux sauvages en pleine nature dans un décore époustouflant ! Nous avons aussi visité un village Massaï et c''était une expérience très enrichissante. Notre guide et notre chauffeur était super et nous avons beaucoup appris sur les animaux, sur Zanzibar et la Tanzanie en général. Nous avons séjourné deux nuits dans un hôtel à Mikumi, le personnel était adorables et aux petits soins pour nous. J''ai été surprise aussi de la modernité du train et sa ponctualité ! Un grand merci à Alfa, Mundir et toute l''équipe. A bientôt

## Review 509

I had such a beautiful day in Zanzibar thanks to my wonderful guide Amina and driver Jingga Boy. Amina made me feel safe, welcome, and truly cared for throughout the whole tour. She shared the fascinating history of Stone Town with so much passion, and I loved how she brought every corner of the city to life with her stories.

Prison Island was a dream—Amina was so attentive and kind, especially when we visited the giant tortoises, a highlight for me!

Later, at Tangawizi Spice Farm, she made the experience fun and interactive. I learned so much, laughed a lot, and even got to taste and smell so many wonderful things.

Jingga Boy was the perfect driver—calm, friendly, always on time, and his driving was smooth and safe. I felt really looked after.

This day will stay with me for a long time. Thank you, Amina and Jingga Boy, for making it so special. I couldn’t have asked for better company.

## Review 514

Take enough money for all the tipping required.

good and interesting day tour; be prepared to hand out a few tips along the route; guide was friendly and professional but mostly left us to read alone or proceed certain areas on our own; friendly and good driver; good vibes

## Review 519

We embarked on a transformative journey through Zanzibar''s vibrant history, culture, and natural wonders with the Private Tour of Stone Town, the Spice Farm, and Prison Island. Led  Ibrahim we explored the ancient streets of Stone Town, adorned with intricate architecture and steeped in tales of centuries past. The we delved into the aromatic world of spices at a traditional Spice Farm, where we were enchanted by the scents of cinnamon, clove, and cardamom. We concluded our adventure on Prison Island, a secluded paradise brimming with giant tortoises.

## Review 520

We had one day from Nungwi Beach to see Zanzibar city. Abraham was awesome!!! He was punctual, extremely friendly and accommodating for our group of 7. He had a great sense of humor and it was like traveling with a friend of the family. He made sure we saw everything we wanted to see and even stayed a little longer so that we could see everything. We and our kids loved Abraham spirit. I would highly recommend this tour and especially Abraham!!

Sehr netter und gesprächiger Guide, hat viel erklärt über die Orte und Kultur. Es war ein sehr schöner Tag auch der Fahrer war sehr zuvorkommend. Der Guide bei der Spice Farm war auch sehr nett und kannte sich gut aus super erklärt.

## Review 523

Wonderful tour with Hamed (Mardi). He was very knowledgeable about the history of Stone Town and prison island. He allowed us ample time to enjoy the town and shop for souvenirs. The guide at the spice farm was also incredible. Wonderful tour.

## Review 524

Evrything was well organized. The guide (Hassan) was very helpful, and very flexible. He answered all the questions and gave assistance when we needed.

## Review 525

this is a taxi driver taking you to the 3 locations. they changed the time of pickup for 2hours later. this was 2hrs away from the hotel and we were taken by a taxi. the 1st location was the cave and we were told we would have 2hrs there. After 1hr the driver told us we had to go. the next location was the beech. this was only 15miles away but took almost 1.5hrs to get there as the taxi wasn''t able to go over the terrain and could only go walking speed to get it it. when we arrived the tide was fully in and we couldn''t even go onto the beech.(if we had left at the original time we would have been on the beech before the tide came in) The 3rd location was at the same point, a small restaurant on the hill next to the beech. Again this was a 5min quick drink and away. (both were meant to be 2hrs) then a 3hr drive  back to the hotel. All in all, if you are looking to spend the whole day in a taxi for just over 1hr at a cave pool then this is a trip for you. Everyone else, stay well clear of this as it was a disaster.

## Review 526

I had sent a lot of time reviewing the many spice farm options, as I was not looking for the tourist version, or being asked to purchase other products/ services. I wanted to immerse myself in the spices, which is what Zanzibar is known for. The tour operator was 30 minutes late. I had to reach out a number of times to find out what time he was expecting to arrive (I understand traffic issues, however the driver could have contacted me). The driver dropped me at the tour and said he would return in 2 hours. Upon completing the tour, another driver picked me up. The tour itself was average. The guide was very informative about the spices that were located on the property. The property is set up for tourist and had minimal of each spice. Too many times there was constant multiple upselling to eat lunch at the property, buy soaps and other products and watch a gentleman climbing a coconut tree. As a solo traveler, I felt uncomfortable with the upselling as I was not able to deflect attention to others. I left informed about some key Zanzibar spices but underwhelmed as I have been wanting to visit the spice farms for many years.

## Review 527

Our tour guide Amina and driver Simba were delightful. Engaging, knowledgeable and professional. The horse safari was amazing as were the 2 horse handlers. The turtle sanctuary was lovely too.

## Review 528

But the price included the admission AND a water taxi! For my party of 5, that was $105 we paid for something the tour operator never did with us. I reached out to the tour operator AND Viator, for a refund of that portion, and crickets.
