Abuu, our guide was friendly and knowledgeable, and he adjusted things as we went based on what we wanted to do. The visit to Prison Island was short and felt a bit rushed, but it was a highlight. Abuu knew a lot about the history of StoneTown, so we really enjoyed learning from him and lunch was delicious. We had a different guide at the spice farm, and while our visit was great and very informational, there were parts that felt like they were just seeking money. A huge bonus was that this tour included a ride to and from our hotel (which was a long way!).

## Review 534

Abu and his driver provided a wonderful tour for us. Abu is knowledgeable and very proud of his country and was excited to share it with us.  He was very accommodating and tailored the tour to our needs. We had a wonderful Swahili lunch even though Abu could not eat because of Ramadan. He arranged a driver for us to take us to dinner at night.  We very much enjoyed our tour with Abu.

## Review 535

Abu was a friendly and informative guide. The tour will benefit from the reopening of the museum. The lunch of assorted carbohydrates was not for the gastronome I am sure there more interesting forms of Swahili food

## Review 536

1 Stern für die Fahrt vom und zum Hotel.
1 Stern für die Spice Farm. 

Wir wurden pünktlich ahgeholt. Die Prison Island ist eine Touristenhochburg. HORROR. Die Schildkröten werden überhaupt nicht artengerecht gehalten. Uns wurde es wähend der Tour durch die Steinstadt zu warm. Deshalb haben wir um 11.45 gebeten das Mittagessen vorzuverschieben. Wie in der Buchungsbestätigung beschrieben ist Lunch ab 12 Uhr. Leider wurde uns dann gesagt, dass Mittagessen erst ab 12.45/13.00 möglich ist. Wir haben dann gesagt, dass wir dies überspringen. Anschliessend habe ich den Organiser gebeten und den Lunch zurückzuerstatten, da 330 USD ohne Lunch und ohne Getränk doch sehr teuer ist. Dieser stellt sich quer und behauptet das sei nicht möglich und wir könnten das Mittagessen an einem anderen Tag durchführen. Für diesen Preis verlangen wir eine gewisse Flexibilität in den vom dem Organisator genannten Zeiten. Komplette Geldverschwendung!

## Review 537

My wife and I enjoyed our day visit to Zanzibar. This tour of the Maganga Spice Farm, Stone Town and Prison Island provided an eclectic overview of the island, its history and people. Abu, our tour guide, was engaging, knowledgeable and personable. He provided excellent information and patiently answered our questions. We would recommend the tour.

## Review 538

I requested Abuu as my tour guide because he received such positive reviews.  I was looking forward to spending the day with him and enjoying this tour.  Unfortunately, it began storming while I was at the spice farm and it rained for a long time. This significantly impacted my time on this tour. 

Once I arrived at the meeting place to meet Abuu, we grabbed umbrellas and I was able to see some of Stone Town.  I enjoyed walking through the narrow streets with him and learning the history and watching people interact with me and each other.

I was not able to go to Prison Island to see the tortoises, which was a huge disappointment, but beyond our control.  I didn''t have lunch in Stone Town either, due, I''m assuming, to the rain.  The spice tour was okay but was the part of the tour I was least excited about.

## Review 539

This was a fabulous tour but what made it so special was our tour guide Abu. 
His chasima and knowledge was excellent and he made the tour so enjoyable. 
I would highly recommend it and ask for Abu. He made all the difference.

## Review 540

I highly recommend this tour. Abu was such an amazing guide! So knowledgeable and kind with a great sense of humor. Traveling alone, safety is a big concern for me but I felt so well taken care of every step of the way. Lunch at the local restaurant gave me the chance to try some new delicious foods I probably wouldn''t have discovered on my own. Overall, it was a fun day and a great way to see some highlights of Zanzibar.

## Review 541

It was good to see spices in their raw form, how is grown, harvested, and tastes befit hitting the store shelves

## Review 542

This tour included everything that we would want to see on Zanzibar and was very well organized to make the most efficient use of our time. Our guide, Abu, was knowledgeable and well prepared and even brought us to the airport at the end. I highly recommend this tour!

## Review 543

This tour was an amazing experience! My tour guide, Abu, who happens to be the owner was very knowledgeable, attentive, and just all around fun! I felt very safe with him as this tour was experienced solo. Prison island, the spice tour, and the delicious local Swahili lunch were my top three favorites! Also, shouts out to the awesome assistant whom also was an excellent guide with great sense of humor!

## Review 544

My tour guide Abu went out his way to meet me at the ferry once I arrived from Dar Es Salaam.  He then guide me on a solo tour throughout Zanzibar.   We started at a Spice Farm, took a boat to Prison Island and ended up walking around Zanzibar before heading to a local spot for lunch. Overall it was a very impressive tour condensed so I could see everything before the last ferry leaves.  I would highly recommend and they suit the tour based on the customer which I appreciate.

## Review 545

Abu was an excellent guide.  He was friendly and very knowledgeable.  We loved the Spice farm and all the variety of spices growing in Zanzibar.  Thank You Abu.

## Review 546

It was a marvellous experience. Our tour guide was superb. We loved every moment and his explanations were very helpful.

## Review 547

It was informative and fun. Our guide Mady was very knowledgable and funny. We loved the spice farm tour and lunch at Jambo but beware the toilet facilities is terrible and dirty! The Stonetown walk was also informative but we had to pay extra for entrance into the Freddy Mercury Museum although the tour info said all entrance fees are included in the price. The prison island trip was a bit of a letdown as its only the turtles that is worth seeing. Also you should take extra water as you only get 1 tiny bottle per person with lunch for the whole day! Also EVERYBODY wants tips, be prepared. Wear comfortable shoes! RNot sure if its worth the price we paid, but did enjoy the tour.

## Review 548

Amazing tour, had a great day exploring the spice farm, prison island and stone town. Saw lots of huge turtles 🐢

## Review 549

Abkar was a wonderful guide! I learned so much about the history of Zanzibar. He was the perfect companion for the day - he balanced giving me information and also allowing me to quietly experience each place we went. It was a long day but I would not have wanted anyone else to guide me through. I felt comfortable asking (many) questions, and I also felt safe and well taken care of. Thank you Abkar!

## Review 550

I highly recommend this tou! Abkar was our guide for this wonderful tour. First, we stopped at Jambo Spice Farm, where we were treated with an informative tour of thw farm,  but also surprised with authentic local cuisine that included fresh spices amd some hand made gifts. We purchased several spices and tea mixes from their huge selection to take home with us. We then went to Stone Town and weaved through the bustling open market, toured a historic church and former slave trading post. Abkar shared plenty of history for each stop. Although it was not part of the tour, we made a stop to shop for tanzanite jewelry as a surprise for our 3 daughters.. we appreciated Abkar''s patience as we compared jewelry at 3 different stores. After that, it was off to prison island, which exceeded our expectations. There were so many friendly tortoises for us to interact with. We had as much time as we wanted to take pictures and interact with these gentle, beautiful creatures, one of which we learned was 200 years old. We also took many pictures of the incredible views from the island looking into Zanzibar City. I''ll also note that the tour company was accommodating and responsive prior to our trip. For an additional cost, they were able to arrange transportation to/from Riu Jambo hotel in Nungwi Beach, which was not one of the pickup points. They also allowed us to push the trip back by 2 days due to last minute illness that I notified them about 4 hours before our pickup time. This was much appreciated, since it fell outside Viator''s 24 hour cancellation window.

## Review 551

I had such a wonderful day with Ismael on this tour. We started with the spice farm where I learned so much about Zanzibar’s spices and got to eat delicious pilau rice. Ismael was very knowledgeable and explained things very well. We then went on a tour of stone town and learned about the history of the East African slave trade and got to see the markets. Finally ended at prison island and got to spend time with the turtles and other animals on the island. I really appreciate Ismael’s explanations of everything, made my experience 10x better.

## Review 552

Très intéressant,  nous avons appris beaucoup de choses différentes aussi bien au niveau de la culture des épices que de l''histoire de Zanzibar. Il y a plusieurs guides, pas de soucis, vous êtes pris en charge et à chaque étape.

## Review 553

I really enjoyed this tour and we got to see a lot.  However, my only compliant is that it seemed a little overpriced for what was provided.  It was advertised as '9 hours'" however the tour didn''t start until 9AM and we finished early.  Also

## Review 554

When booking the tour months ago., Viator advertised it as “reserve now - credit card will be charged 24 hours before tour commences”. In actuality, Viator charged the credit card at the time of booking. Between the time of booking and the actual tour, Viator “amended” the tour, but did not give specifics as to what was “amended”.  On the date the tour, two of the included attractions for which entry tickets were originally included were closed.  No credit given.  When the tour was booked, the tour included entry tickets to the Freddie Mercury Museum.  On the date of the tour, we had to pay the entry fee ($16 USD for two tickets). No credit given.  Tour was led by Mady.  A trainee guide also participated. When the trainee guide attempted to lead a portion of the tour, Mady had to take over because of the trainee’s lack of knowledge and proper English.  Mady ended his tour leadership at the beach where we were led by the trainee and boarded the boat (which was not outfitted with life jackets) to cross the open Indian Ocean (where both large ships and speedy boats did cause sizable waves in relation to the size of our boat) to Prison Island.  Trainee, yet again, demonstrated lack of knowledge and proper English.  Mady was at the beach when we returned. Mady asked how we enjoyed the tour of Prison Island. We shared it did not go well and that the trainee needed more training. Mady told the trainee, then and there in front of us, what we said.  We tipped Mady $40 USD on top of $280 USD we paid. Mady barely acknowledged the tip and did not look us in the eye when he accepted it. The tour was advertised as pick-up and drop off at same location (our hotel).  Mady attempted to get us to walk back to our hotel from the beach market unaccompanied. We told Mady that was unacceptable.  Finally, the trainee did accompany us back to our hotel. Lunch at the Spice Farm included a very small piece of cooked/cold/breaded fish, a very small piece of cooked/cold/bony chicken and lots of carbs and vegetables.  Be mindful that everyone (tour leader, trainee, taxi driver, spice farm guide, space farm dessert preparer, etc.) is looking for a tip which we paid.  The Spice Farm was the most interesting.

## Review 555

The tour guide was well informed about the island. He made sure we understood the history of Zanzibar and its culture. It was a fun and rewarding experience.

## Review 556

Hassan gives a very interesting tour and has extensive knowledge about Tanzanian history, culture and language. I recommend his tour!

## Review 557

The spice tour was very interesting. It was awesome to see how day-to-day ingredients are grown - this is true especially for those who are into cooking. Our guide was knowledgeable and made it fun for us. They treated us to a tasty lunch after - glad we did this

Prison Island lacked depth. Though, it was nice to be on the water for a bit during the day. The tortoises were fun. And, the island itself is pretty. So, no complaints here

The walking tour was terrific. Our guide was super knowledgeable and truly passionate about teaching us. This was particularly impressive. It was just the guide, myself, and my wife. So, we were able to have a very natural dialogue where we were able to ask a ton of questions. This was the highlight of the day

## Review 558

Ismail was a great guide, we really enjoyed the Spice Farm, and had a very nice lunch. We walked around in the town after our visit to the Spice Farm and were able to visit the Anglican Church and the Cathedral and learn all about the Slave Trade and of course we went to the Freddie Mercury Museum, being huge Queen fans. To top off a great day we went across to Prison Island and saw the huge Tortoises, which was an amazing experience!

## Review 559

Our tour guide was excellent, we really enjoyed our tour to Prison island and the spice tour, we arrived in Zanzibar by cruise ship, my mother is 80 plus and they made sure that we both had a great time, the tour giude helped me with getting her on/off the small wooden boat to Prison Island- highly recommended company.

## Review 560

Enjoyed spice tour did not get to visit prison. City tour was done with out explination. I was not very hsppy.🤥

## Review 561

Very nice tour to know more about Stone Town. First is the spice farm, than walking tour and finally Prision Island. Mady is the perfect guide for the walking tour and Prision Island. Very passionate about Zanzibar history which makes much more fun to learn more about it. P.S. nothing to do with the tour company - to those who are going for this tour take the chance to go to the Prision Island reception and ask them for a bigger cage for the baby turtles; there is a reason they need to be in cages - which Mady can explain - but they definitely could have a bigger cage with all the space in the island.

## Review 562

This was a fantastic experience and I highly recommend it to anyone who is visiting Zanzibar and wants to immerse themselves in its rich heritage and history. The Spice Tour was both an education and lots of fun. The highlight for me was the Stone Town Tour. My guide, Masoud, was extremely knowledgeable about Zanzibar''s political, social and cultural history and made the whole experience very entertaining and engaging. The tour operator was very accommodating and understanding and I thoroughly enjoyed the day I had in Zanzibar. I heartily thank everyone who made the day what it was.

## Review 563

It was a really great experience. Our guide was really illustrative, well comprehensive, a fantastic person with knowledge about history of this beautiful country. Very professionalism.

## Review 564

Attraction à faire, on découvre différents espèces d''épices et comment les utiliser, notre guide nous expliquait tout en détail et les bien faits que cela peux procurer sur notre santé avec une bonne utilisation. Cela a été très enrichissant ,sans oublier la forêt de jozani avec ses magnifiques singes colobus

## Review 565

Tour said it was from 8-12, and listed states we would visit Michamvi Kae Beach. Both of these were not true. We were later told our pickup would be 9am, after we messaged to be sure of timing. So we waited. At 9:30, we messaged again. No response. At 9:45 we messaged. No response. At around 10, we connected with Viator for a refund. This was 2 hours behind where we originally expected the tour to start. They finally showed up and said they got turned around and couldn’t find our hotel. Even though we sent them a pin of our location. Very odd. Other guests in the car said they’d been driving in circles for a long time. We finally arrive at boat spot, get snorkel gear figured out then set off for starfish. Not Michamvi Kae Beach. Just a spot off the shore a little ways near Pingwe. After 20 mins here, off to Blue Lagoon, a spot off shore between Pingwe and Dongwe. Great fish, very packed with boats and snorkelers. Snorkel gear provided was pretty rough, so we quit early, because masks leaked, and hurt our faces. Finally off to third stop, The Rock Restaurant. We walk down from the beach we started at. Low tide, so lots of walking. We get to restaurant and they ask for our booking, so we show them the Viator Booking. Ah, we find out we don''t have a real reservation with the restaurant. This is NOT included in the tour. Something we needed to arrange ourselves apparently? We didn''t know. Thankfully, restaurant gets this a lot and understood, and made space for us. The Rock is fun to visit, but pricey, and small portions. We are then asked to hurry, as the other guests want to leave for their hotel. We do, and then find out they took a different shuttle. Tour ended around 2:30pm. Ridiculous. Almost a week later, and we still await our refund! DO NOT RECOMMEND THIS TOUR!!

## Review 566

Una estafa para lo ofrecido. Contratamos guia en español y no sólo q no sabia hablar sino que no explico nada. Hicimos snorkel se veia 1 sola variedad de peces observables desde el barco. El lugar de las estrellas de mar insignificante y decia banco de arena que nunca fuimos!

## Review 567

The experience was great. The guide Khamis and driver Yssup Bayo were then exceptional. They made the experience even more magical for my wife and I. 10/10

## Review 568

Our driver MAISARA picked us up from our hotel on time and the car was immaculate. He was very pleasant.
On the way we picked up our guide for the day YAKOUB. 
After joining with our boat operator, we set off to see the star fish.   They were beautiful, large and markings were like jewels.
Next stop was snorkelling, although there was snorkelling equipment available, we used our own, not sure there were life jackets.
I found it quite difficult to climb in and out of the boat, it was my husband who helped me.
Final stop was The Rock restaurant, if we hadn’t booked a table the trip would have been very disappointing.  Fortunately I had booked online and the very welcoming staff at The Rock attended to us. We thoroughly enjoyed our experience there, it made the trip.
This is not included in your trip cost and is absolutely worth doing.   A small ferry transported us back to the shore and we met up with our driver to return to our hotel.

## Review 569

Worst excursion ever.  Everything about it was sketchy.  Picked up by two questionable men who were vague about the trip, took us to some off road parking lot type of place full of plastic trash bottles and local men rummaging around.  Not a tourist in site and did NOT feel safe.  We get out and start walking to follow our  “guide” to an unknown destination through more trash and ramshackle.  End up at some funky shack to get poor condition, dirty snorkeling gear and only one of us gets fins that remotely fit, the other too large.  They start filling plastic containers with gasoline which is spilling out and on the ground.  Frightening.  Ask to use a bathroom and he says there is none unless I pay $1.  I had to use it.  Weird.  We think about leaving but are too scared and keep thinking this is Viator, a company we have always only had great experiences with.  Then follow our sketchy “guide” about 1/2 hour on foot to a dingy boat which was basically a tin can with a wooden plank on the bottom with a guy in it.  Bang on the waves out to “the blue lagoon” which was anything but a lagoon but at least there are other tourists in similar boats.  We are too afraid they will take off with our stuff if we both get in the water so I stay in the boat while my daughter daughter snorkels.  She does see some great fish but lots of naive tourists standing on the coral killing it with no one instructing to get off.

## Review 570

This trip was too expensive for what was provided. First the tour was supposed to be private but we found to more tourists in the car with us. The guide´s English was not very good at all, not too easy to comunicate.  No water was provided during the whole tour. The snorchling was hardly average, the star fish were stunning and beautiful. Lunch at the Rock restaurand was overprised but worth the experience. Overall - too expensive for too little.

## Review 571

The sea stars where amazing, it was a pitty that we good not eat at the Rock restaurant. The snorkeling at the rif was not spectaculair

## Review 572

The guide was polite and friendly '' but is vers boring at the Jozani forest , the rock restaurant was not to recommande the food was not our taste

## Review 573

La recogida puntual.
Cogimos una barca desde stone town y nos fuimos hacia la isla de la prision, el guía hizo una breve explicación y tuvimos tiempo para ver y hacer fotos de las tortugas, luego volvimos a coger la barca y a unos 100m se paró el motor, que después de varios intentos y ir a la deriva consiguió arrancar…
En stone town volvimos a coger el coche y nos fuimos al bosque Jozani, que el recorrido apenas duró 20 minutos, nos quedamos con ganas de ver más monos, luego fuimos a comer en un restaurante de Paje donde no pudimos bañarnos porque teniamos que ir rápido al restaurante the rock sin explicación ninguna del guía, y lo poco que sabia de español lo básico, mientras nos haciamos fotos el se hiba a hablar y a jugar con sus amigos.
Se ven demasiadas cosas en un solo dia y todo se ve muy rápido sin tiempo de disfrutar de cada lado. Y muchas horas de coche.

## Review 574

I really enjoyed this experience. I met up with a guide at Forodhani Market, who then brought me to a larger group who I spent the rest of the tour with. We paid the tax to go to the island before getting the boat to Prison Island and then we paid the entry fee to the island separately when we got to Prison Island. Our guide took us around the island, explaining its history and we had the chance to explore the prison for about 30-minutes before we went to visit the giant turtles for about another 30-minutes, which was a perfect amount of time for each activity. After Prison Island, we got the boat to Nakupenda where we were given the chance to wander around for about 45-minutes (it''s a sandbank so it isn''t very big) before being called for lunch. Lunch was a huge spread of seafood including prawns, octopus, langoustine and octopus alongside rice, chips, a tomato sauce and a drink. In the end, we had about 2 hours here, which I felt was perfect. I was travelling solo, and all the other guests were couples, however, the long tour meant we had the chance to talk and get to know one another. Also, while at Nakupenda, another solo traveller joined us who I ended up exploring the town with after the tour had ended, so I''d recommend this tour for solo travellers as it''s a good way to meet some other people who are travelling.

## Review 575

After taking his tour, I can see why Hassan is so highly regarded on TripAdvisor. 

We had a great tour of Stone Town with Hassan. I appreciated his communication the day before and morning of, and his promptness - we arrived early to the meeting point and he was already there waiting. 

Hassan has an immense knowledge about Stone Town that he is passionate about sharing. We learned all about Zanzibar’s history. 

I am also thankful for Hassan’s tips for safety, shopping, and the night market.

## Review 576

Our tour was great overall. The pace was nice, very informative, our guide gave us options to stop at places that interested us or move past throughout the duration of the tour. He also really knew his stuff, impressive knowledge of the island history.

## Review 577

Hassan was very knowledgeable and took us to many areas within Stone Town, really bringing us into the heart of Zanzibar’s culture. He was incredibly kind and helpful throughout the tour. I would definitely recommend him to friends, family, and other guests who want to explore Stone Town. Thank you again, Hassan!

## Review 578

I did this tour as a solo traveller and ended up having a completely private tour with just myself and the guide, Hassan. He was fantastic — relaxed, flexible, and very knowledgeable about the area. He gave me a great introductory history of Stone Town along with interesting insights into life in Zanzibar.

We visited several key sites, including historic buildings, the Freddie Mercury Museum, the Anglican Cathedral, and the spice markets. There was also time for me to explore some touristy shops and experience the local markets. (Just a heads-up: the fish market is very fishy! Hassan warned me, but it was still more intense than I expected! 🤣)

A note for other solo travellers: the app says it’s suitable for individuals, which it definitely is, but booking as one person doesn’t guarantee you’ll join a group. In my case, it was just me — which turned out to be a really nice, personalised experience.

Overall, this was a highly rated trip for a reason — great value and an excellent guide. I’d absolutely recommend it.

## Review 579

We had a great tour of stone town with Hassan; super informative without being boring, Hassan knows a lot about all the buildings we’ve visited, but is also happy to share a lot of knowledge about Zanzibars history, politics and life as a zanzibarian in general as well, flexible, not too long. Can 100% recommend :)

## Review 580

We had an excellent visit the guide was so knowledgable and we enjoyed learning about the different spices and their uses. The jewellery made of the leaves and flowers was amazing. The addition of trying a range of delicious fresh fruit and watching a young man climb the tree to retrieve a coconut and then drinking the milk and eating the fresh coconut was a lovely addition and made the whole afternoon very enjoyable. A great visit would highly recommend

## Review 581

This was one of the most interesting experiences I had during my time in Tanzania. I really enjoyed learning about the different spices and how they were/continue to be used and found the guides very informative.

## Review 582

Very interesting tour. We got a lot of useful information.

## Review 583

This tour was absolutely amazing from start to finish! Our driver, Yussuf, reached out to us well before the tour even started, answering all of our questions and making sure everything was set. Once he picked us up, we met Khamis, who gave us a fantastic, clear brief of the day''s itinerary and happily answered all of our remaining questions before we headed toward the beach.
At the beach, we met our captain, Mzii, who took us out toward Mnemba Island. Chasing the dolphins to capture them on my GoPro video was so much fun! Mzii was incredible and even managed to capture some amazing, up-close footage of the dolphins for us. Afterward, we relaxed on a stunning sandbank beach, enjoying fresh fruit in the sun. Mzii then brought us back to Khamis, who treated us to a delicious lunch.
To top it all off, we headed to the aquarium to feed the sea turtles, which was a wonderful highlight. Overall, this was an extremely adventurous and well-organized tour. Having three dedicated guides, Yussuf, Khamis, and Mzii, take care of us made the experience feel so personalized and special. I highly recommend this tour to anyone visiting the island!

## Review 584

I was very disappointed with this tour. The driver did not do his job well at all. He seemed unprepared and uninterested, and he failed to provide any explanation about the places we visited. A tour is supposed to be informative and engaging, but instead, we were left confused and frustrated. The lack of communication made the experience feel like just a long car ride rather than a guided tour. For the price we paid, I expected much better service. I would not recommend this tour to others.

## Review 585

I didn''t like, not recommended at all I didn''t like, not recommended at all   I didn''t like, not recommended at all

## Review 586

Grosse grosse déception !

Habitué des excursions par Viator et très souvent satisfait des offres proposées, celle-ci était un vrai désastre sur le plan de l’organisation, de la prestation malgré un prix bien plus cher que le marché (6 personnes).
Malgré l’heure de la prise en charge respectée (seul point positif), la personne yussuf (je crois car il ne s’est pas présenté mais en lui a demandant lors de nos échanges et il semblait répondre oui....) qui nous a accompagné tout au long du parcours ne semblait pas du tout impliqué sur l’encadrement et encore moins bavard, à aucun moment il nous a adressé la parole et nos questions restaient sans réponse durant les différents trajets (anglais comme français). 
Sur le plan de l’excursion, le bateau proposé semblait être pris au 1er venu sur la plage (sans gilet de sauvetage), fort heureusement la traversée s’est bien déroulée près de l’île de Mnemba pour effectuer un peu de snorking accompagné d’un équipage d’adolescents sans une réèelle implication à notre égard avec à priori un guide (parlant soit disant français) sans vraiment d’explications sur les lieux et qui semblait aussi être en vacances tout comme nous…

Après un retour sur terre, direction le déjeuner dans une sorte de restaurant près d’une route fréquentée et dont la cuisine à proximité était dans une cabane sous quelques feuilles de cocotiers, cela semblait très douteux (bref très local) et avec un service d’1h30 d’attente puis à l’arrivée presque pas assez à déjeuner pour le groupe de 6 personnes (loin des photos du dépliant).

Pour la suite, petite halte à l’aquarium Nungwi (sans vraiment d’intérêt à part peut-être s’approcher des très belles tortues) mais dommage que le lieu soit si mal organisé envahi par une sur-population touristique dans une eau peu attirante (encore très loin du bleu du dépliant).

Ensuite le « super guide et son chauffeur » nous a déposés (sans aucune explication ) sur la plage de kendwa (très jolie place) mais à 15h pour un couché de soleil à 18h30 avec seulement une toute petite partie de sable car cette plage est réservée de part et d’autres aux hôtels. Du coup, après un plouf, nous avons demandé à retourner à notre hôtel pour abréger cette décevante excursion.

En bref, je ne recommande absolument pas cette excursion par ce biais, dommage car plusieurs personnes autours de nous ont fait la même excursion ou equivalent via l’hôtel (d’autres contacts) et c’était beaucoup mieux.

## Review 587

Very polite and friendly driver and tour guide. As this is a private tour you are in charge of how much time you want to spend at each location. Food was amazing and we always got cold water. Air conditioned car was life saver. Trip to the island was brilliant and snorkeling was best on Zanzibar. Be prepared to be active and have reasonable mobility. Was great for me and my brother, however my elderly mother struggled as there was a lot of stairs and uneven walking. The staff however made sure that she has extra support. Be prepared to pay extra for activities like jet ski etc.

## Review 588

Great tour. We were able to see the dolphins and play the chase game with them. Our tour guide was very nice.

## Review 589

We had a really nice experience and really felt like a private tour, from pick up to visiting all the locations. Our guide left us enjoy the attractions without any kind of pressure and we communicated via WhatsApp. He was very punctual, arrived at our pick up location 5 minutes early and waited for us, he even gave us a heads up before arriving.

## Review 590

Getting to the snorkeling spot takes more than two hours (car + boat), snorkeling itself lasted about half an hour. There are a lot of other boats and tourists at the snorkeling spot. There are very few corals, the fish are quite ordinary, few species. But all this would not have spoiled the impression so much if it were not for the terrible organization. There were no life jackets on the boat (that is, about two hours, there and back, clients travel without any protection). According to the organizer, snorkeling lasts 'as long as you want'"

## Review 591

We had a great time on our tour. Yussuf was brilliant and contacted us just after booking to introduce himself and arrange pick up.

We saw dolphins and had chance to jump in and see them in the water. The snorkelling was great and I’ve never seen the sea so blue.

Overall the day was brilliant and would recommend.

This trip was a disaster! Would not recommend.

The guide Alex was dismissive of our complaints saying that nakupenda is just a sandbank, and we tried to say we have paid £160 for the 2 of us to go on this trip to prison island and nakupenda which should be about 6 hours long. ## Review 639

It was boring (except for the tortoises) and hot. The tour could have been so much better. Our guide was not really into it and he was very hard to understand. Plus we started with one guide and were handed off to another. The booking with Viator included the guide but you need cash for tips (boat ride) and entrance fees to Prison Island, Slave Museum, and the Sultan''s grave. The guide took us through the food market and was very considerate in asking first if we are OK to walk by the meat and fish. We did, and I lost my appetite! Instead of a neighborhood food spot that the guide recommended, we opted for air conditioning and lunch at the Park Hyatt. Stone Town has a real appeal to it but does not deliver unfortunately. They have not maintained their history well and our guide did not help us tap into the vibe. Wandering the alleyways on our own was more fun.

I recommend this tour 1000%

## Review 641

September 2024 ‘Stone Town and Prison Island Private Tour Zanzibar’ with Lazuli Tours - DO NOT BOOK THIS!! We paid $249.39 (for two people) for what should have been a 5 hour tour. The Stone Town potion should have been in an air conditioned vehicle with water provided. Instead we were picked up by foot by a guide (who was 15mins late) and walked to the boat to go to Prison Island. When we returned to Stone Town we were handed over to another guide who informed us the tour was walking in 30+ degree heat. Nowhere in the tour description did it say the tour was a walking tour. The guide had absolutely no specialist knowledge and the entire tour took about 3.5 hours. When the tour company owner was questioned about the tour description he blamed Viator and Viator blamed the tour company. The tour operator said the air conditioned vehicle part of description was for pick up and drop off - we were not picked up or dropped off in a vehicle. We asked for a partial refund which had to be agreed by the tour company which of course they rejected. DON’T WASTE MONEY WITH THIS TOUR- JUST MAKE THE ARRANGEMENTS YOURSELF!

The boat ran out of gas on the way back from prison island. So, we had to hop on another boat that already had some guests in it. An amazing experience visiting Jozani Forest, The Rock Restaurant, Kuza Cave, and Paje Beach! Our tour guide was excellent knowledgeable, friendly, and very accommodating.

## Review 674

Myself and my husband had a fantastic time on the Jozani Forest, Kuza Cave, Paje Beach and Rock restaurant trip.

Our guide and driver were already waiting for us at our meeting point when we got there. The private car was very nice and so comfortable. Our guide talked about Zanzibar and was so informative with every place that we visited, and very engaging. Although the itinerary had set times for each place, our guide asked if we preferred more time in one place and less time in another so that we had the right time for us in each place, which we loved. He also managed to find a big family of red Colobus Monkey’s as he knew their behaviours and where to look. 

The only downside for us was Kuza Cave. There were no changing rooms and the toilets were filthy. We were expected to change inside the cave in front of everyone else, which we were not comfortable with. The area inside the cave is very small, so you are on top of everyone. There are also insects in the water that bite so we didn’t spend long in the water. We feel it would be better for the company to choose a different cave like Malum cave, which is better set up for tourists.

## Review 678

Great tour! Our guide did a great job finding the Red Colobus Monkeys and showing us around the mangroves. We were skeptical about Kuza cave but once we saw it and got in the water it erased any doubt. Water was absolutely lovely and they have a changing area prior to heading down. The cave does have some stairs but the final parts will require some careful footing to get into the water. Temp was warmer than the ocean and absolutely refreshing. Also ate at The Rock, great experience but you''ll need to make a reservation yourself separately.  Just let the guide and driver know, they made sure we got there with a few minutes to spare (we did 1400 to give us enough time with the forest, cave, and Paje). Paje Beach was okay for some quick pictures but if you don''t have lunch reservations at the Rock there looks like a number of options there but you''ll have to select/find something yourself. Our tour had a guide and driver so plan cash tips appropriately.

Lovely setting, hot but white sand beach and blue sea. Then off to Kuza limestone cave with staligtites and a clear water pond at the bottom. L ´équipe ( guide et chauffeur) a été formidable : le guide Abrar parle très bien le français et prend plaisir à nous faire découvrir son île et le chauffeur SaId est très prévenant L excursion mérite le détour avec la découverte de la mangrove après l’observation des singes en forêt avant la merveilleuse plage de Paje pour terminer par un déjeuner plus que parfait à The Rock ! 😍

## Review 696

This was a really nice experience. My husband and I enjoyed the views. We ate at the Rock restaurant which was good.

## Review 697

It was a good experience. The driver was pleasant, on time and safe. We spent a very short period in the forest.

## Review 698

First : about this tour.
The driving 🚦 
The driving was really bad
Although this is not my first long ride in Zanzibar so I know some roads aren''t that good
But the driver was really bad that we suffered all the time that we were in the road, which caused us a headache.
The police stopped us because he was speeding.

Jozani forest:
We saw monkeys and the tour was amazing and the guide were able to explain all the details about the forest for us.

Kuza cave:
The place is not worth visiting, and the guide left us there and said once you are done come back to the car! 
I also asked for a lifejacket but he didn''t care much! 
He also didn''t explained anything for us regarding the cave.

Paje beach:
The beach was nice.
I hated the fact that the driver just dropped us there and said “once you are done come back to the car” it was really a bad experience, he didn’t give us any information about anything! Literally anything! 
He didn’t guide us to the lockers or a good restaurants there.
Even when we asked he didn’t know! 

Same thing goes to the Rock
He didn’t have any knowledge about it.

Overall: we didn’t feel really save or taken care of in this ride, I don’t recommend it.

Second: the destinations
About the destination: I would recommend the rock and Jozani Forest.🌳

## Review 699

Very punctual and well organized. The trip was a private tour, so it’s very flexible to spend more time here and less time there depending on my preference. 
Every place I visited was so beautiful and memorable.

## Review 700

Food was delicious with the excellent service they provided! The view was outstanding! Will recommend to anyone

## Review 701

Jozani Forest walk with onsite guide was very informative. It was so hot that the swim in Kuza cave with onsite guide was very refreshing, rough rocks so good to wear shoes. Driver dropped us off at Paje beach, lovely to walk long way out on low tide but don’t linger out there as the tide comes in very fast. Dropped off at The Rock restaurant which was amazing to see. 
Our driver was very safe and courteous but was not a guide. Take money to tip the guides at Jozani and Kuza.

## Review 702

Die Kuza Höhle war sehr schön. Der Tourguide für den Jozani Wald war motiviert und wusste viele spannende Fakten.

## Review 703

My husband and I really enjoyed this excursion! Our driver was amazing. The tour of Jozani Forest was incredible and our guide was really knowledgeable, you can tell all the staff enjoy their jobs. 

We didn’t spend much time at Kuza Cave, but learning the history behind the cave was very interesting. The steps one cave are really steep, almost scary. But once you’re in the water, it’s so refreshing. My husband was even able to play with the drummers at Kuza Cave. 

We only stayed at Paje Beach for about 10 minutes, but I would have loved to see more of it. There were a few interesting restaurants and many vendors selling locally made items. The water was nice and blue too, such a lovely sight! 

Our last stop was at The Rock restaurant. There was the option to make reservations days before the excursion to eat at the restaurant, or simply enjoy drinks and take in the amazing views. We opted to make reservations and had two amazing dishes! The price for so much food was ridiculously inexpensive! 

I highly recommend this excursion, you will not be disappointed!

## Review 704

James was an excellent driver and guide. He took me all over the island and showed me some of the amazing parts of Zanzibar that some don't explore. He was a great driver and ensured that I was comfortable at all times.

## Review 705

I specifically selected this Zan Dreamland Tours tour prior to arriving, as I had only a short stay in Stone Town / Zanzibar. James, my driver and guide for the 8 hour tour, went way above and beyond to ensure that I had a great experience, especially given I was a solo female traveller. Throughout the day, James shared his knowledge and passion for all things Zanzibar and Tanzania. At the Monkey Forest, James handed my “tour” over the to the parks formal guides and I joined a number of other people of a tour that was able to sight both red and blue monkeys as well as an elephant shrew. I must admit, our forest tour guide was very serious and of few words, but it was a good experience. James and I arrived at Kuza Cave, surprisingly to no tourist or other locals in attendance, We were blessed to have 45 minutes without any other visitors. Kuza Cave is truly magical, a hidden gem down a small entrance. One arrival at Paje Beach, I was stunned at the absolute beauty of the view, the water really is that blue and the sands are that white. James provide information on the local beach bios and general information of the area that is Paje Beach. I was able to have some time to walk along the beach prior to lunch. My preference was to eat local, and James was able to take me to a fabulous local diner, where I was kindly and warmly welcomed. Our final destination, the Rock did not disappoint. A genuine five star review. At no time did I feel rushed, in fact James reminded me on a few occasions to pole pole. I had booked other tours prior to arrival, and one of these I wished I had use James and Zan Dreamland Tours. James mentioned that this a relatively new company and I would highly recommend joining one of their tours!

## Review 706

This is a great tour, mainly with the combination of activities is a good mix and not too far away from each other. Because it’s private, you also have a lot of freedom with the driver on when to go/leave individual places which is great. Make sure you book at the rock restaurant separately if you want to eat there and tell the driver your booking time. Also make sure to bring shoes suitable for bush walking (jozani forest) and beaches (rock/ paje).

## Review 707

Me and my friend love this trip. Our driver was very patient and gave us a lot of time on each destination. I highly recommend this trip to everyone visiting Zanzibar.

## Review 708

Kuza cave was my favorite. There was only 2 other people and it was beautiful. I recommend eating in Paje instead of the Rock. Paje was very cute.

## Review 709

Experience was good. Guides excellent. Make sure you wear trainers for rainforest and Kuza cave as very rocky and uneven. Paje beach and Tge Tock stunning

## Review 710

Being able to see a few different places while being based in Stone Town was great. My guide Jumon (hope i spelled his name correctly) was great! Knowledgeable and patient with this traveler. Would recommend

## Review 711

This was a lovely experience despite the rains Mr Hasan was very friendly and being a solo traveler mr Hasan was great company he was very patient and at no point did I feel rushed. I definitely would recommend

## Review 712

I can recommend that tour. It was a very nice day without any hussle. The driver was also my guide he was very friendly, calm and relaxed. We had a nice day and even the short rain could''nt change that :)

## Review 713

Excellente excursion et parfaitement organisée. Récupération a l’hôtel a l’heure prévue et départ pour les visites prévues au programme :
- La forêt de Jozani qui vaut le détour avec la présence de nombreux singes
- Une grotte où il est possible de se baigner
- La plage de Paje, qui est très belle et où il est également possible de se baigner
- Pour finir : le restaurant The Rock qui est magnifique. Si vous souhaitez y déjeuner, pensez à réserver une table sur le site du restaurant a 14h (ça vaut le coup !).

En synthèse : super journée et excursion parfaitement organisée avec un guide francophone. Je recommande !

## Review 714

Everything was great. It was good to get off the resort and see more of Zanzibar. Our tour guide was extremely knowledgeable. I didn't realize the forest was walking through wet ground and rocks so maybe in the description to add to wear sneakers or tennis shoes.

## Review 715

We really enjoyed our day. Zan Dreamland Tours were excellent. We had good communication from Ismail and our driver, Sultan was great. Jozani Forest was delightful, with a knowledgeable guide informing us about the monkeys and then the Mangroves. We loved Kuza Cave and swam there, getting some awesome pics , followed by a relaxed lunch at Paje Beach and then a visit to The Rock to take photos. We felt the day was well balanced and very glad we did the Tour. We will definitely use Zan Dreamland Tours again and in fact used them to take us to the airport .

## Review 716

We enjoyed the trip. Both the guide and driver were good' amiable and very pleasant

## Review 717

Seeing the Zanzibar Red Colobus Monkeys alone was worth the trip - we were able to get so close to them and take so many photos and videos!

## Review 718

Enjoyed this trip for most part. Our guide Juma was very friendly and helpful. We were first taken to the forest. There we were handed over to a local guide from the centre. He was knowledgeable but he at times seemed more interested in his mobile phone than showing us around. Juma next took us to Kuza cave. This was nice and we swam in it briefly. It’s definitely not one for those with mobility issues as the steps down are rocky and steep. There is a cultural centre there too which was enjoyable. Paje beach was very nice and probably the highlight for me. Beautiful beach. We visited the rock restaurant but because we hadn’t known the itinerary for the day and times we had not booked to eat. It was interesting to see but possibly not worth the drive out. Overall a nice trip to see different places on the island.

## Review 719

This was the best tour we did while we stayed in Zanzibar. Our guide gave us enough time to visit each place and to enjoy a lovely lunch at Paje beach. For those of you who are wondering if you should have lunch at Paje beach or at the Rock Restaurant, chose Paje beach- you have a lot of options. The Rock Restaurant is great for photos. In general, this was definitely worth it. We enjoyed it very much.

## Review 720

I am so happy we ended up booking this trip! We loved it! Everything actually exceeded our expectations from the monkeys right through to the Rock restaurant. Highly recommend!

## Review 721

This tour is a great way to see many of the highlights of the island.  My favorite part was seeing the Red Colobus monkeys.  You can see them so close up and we saw some babies too.  Paje beach was also beautiful.

## Review 722

For us Paje beach was the best. Absolutely stunning! We travelled with Zan Dreamland tours nothing a problem. Very courteous driver. Kuza cave is down a bumpy dirt track and the cave itself isn’t accessible for wheelchair users. There was a group of guys who sang a couple of songs which made our day. Really good! If in Zanzibar go to Paje Beach amazing !

## Review 723

Very packed amazing day. Our driver and guide were friendly, knowledgeable, patient and very kind. Must recommend

## Review 724

We wanted to book a tour where we could see many things in one day. This trip did not disappoint!
 Jozani Forest was an amazing experience and the monkeys are adorable, swimming in Kuza Cave was so refreshing and therapeutic, we even had the whole place to our self! 
The beaches that you visit are beautiful with the whitest sand and bluest waters, it was a highlight of our honeymoon.
Our driver arrived on time and drove very carefully on some of the less maintained roads, we felt very safe. He showed us some of the rice and sugar cane fields which we weren’t expecting so that was a nice bonus. The vehicle was air conditioned and in great condition.would definitely recommend booking with this company!

## Review 725

Très belle visite qui nous permet de voir les sites à faire tout en étant accompagné d’un bon guide.

## Review 727

I still enjoyed myself and would totally recommend this tour.

## Review 728

Very good experience. Suliman speak very well Italian language. Bravo suliman .  Jazani forest was very good

## Review 730

## Review 731

Firstly this was good value for money. When one is limited on time these combo deals are just brilliant. Individually the cost is so much higher. Every step of the tour was well planned. Driver arrived on time. Tour guide really experienced, patient and very well mannered. Took time to answer all questions and made sure even our personal needs were met. Like stopping to buy some fruit.
We expected to see more than monkeys in the forest, but perhaps it wasnt our day. the mangrove forests were too intriguing. Enjoyed the teak wood trees as well.
Kuza cave. the guide at Kuza cave was a bit arrogant when asked a question.  But the cave itself is extremely beautiful.   The rock restaurant is rare. Small and quaint and very cosy inside.
Great food. 
Paje beach was really inviting and beautiful. sand was so fine. didn''t see that before

## Review 732

Such an awesome experience. The sandbank was breathtaking, and the beachside lunch with fresh seafood was delicious! They give you a great portion size and continuously make sure you’re well fed and taken care of. There was also local entertainment on the sandbank. The tortoise sanctuary was awesome! We got to also see peacocks and other animals, which wasn’t expected. Then the Stonetown walking tour was the icing one cake. We got to learn so much about Zanzibar’s history and culture. We walked the streets of stone town seeing different architecture and stopped at a spice market where we could purchase spices. This was a 10/10 experience. Our tour guide Hashir was so cute and informative! Very friendly and polite!

## Review 733

Tour guide A.Razak was very professional , he showed and explained every thing , so I don’t have any question ,lunch with seafood was amazing ,fresh fruits as well ,only big issued was Nakupenda sandbank doesnot have any wash room,so around 2 hours staying there without wash room is very difficult ,Highly recommended ,,thanks

## Review 734

We had a wonderful and fun experience on the this tour. Everyone was courteous and professional. They (captain/assistant/tour guide) made us felt safe while on the boat. Abu our tour guide is very knowledgeable, he shared his knowledge about prison island, its history and representation. He ended the tour with the famous Jambo song. Would definitely recommend taking this tour.

## Review 735

Lack of water supplied, no toilet breaks offered, lack of information throughout the tour, no changing facilities, no life jackets or safety information.   Food very disorganised and not enough for party size, if you don’t like seafood only chicken available and very little of it and you can’t mix and match, fish offered was cut in half and if you were lucky you got tail end, unlucky a head 🤢🤮
Boat people were quite entertaining and tried hard but better organisation required stonetown tour disappointing.
Quite expensive for what’s on offer

## Review 736

Mohamed was very friendly and worried at all times that we were enjoying the ride. His Spanish is very good. Highly recommended.

## Review 737

Yassir (tour operator/owner) is good man. He helped me with pick-up and drop-off from/to hotel. Reasonably priced. Picking other guests from different hotels took long time because of some miscommunication but eventually we were a group of 7, Yassir is decent in English, French and Spanish. The prison island boat ride was fun, the prison itself (now a museum and commercial place) is not good (there''s hardly anything to see). Peacocks were good. Too many old turtles on walkways causing BIG crowd and delay and ruining experience a bit. Then Nakupenda - sand bank is amazing, water is cold (not warm like in Caribbean), food mostly catered to non-veg. Seems very commercial and a bit crowded. But good. Stonetown is absolutely overrated in my opinion. There''s nothing to see/experience except small lanes/streets and vendors and hotels and restaurants. Not much ethnic, cultural, historical stuff. A bit dirty and congested. I wish I had not spent time/money on it. Overall, I''d say it was decent tour, I''d recommend company and guide. Appreciation for places lies in eyes of visitor.

## Review 738

It is not a private safari as advertised, it is shared with 20 others, they say it includes alcoholic beverages but in reality it is only a bottle of beer and exclusively at mealtime. The view is beautiful, but the attention is regular and the fact that I thought it was private gave me a very bad time. When we arrived they made us disembark in a different place full of rocks and passing through an area that smelled rotten, we crossed a very ugly area walking to reach the vehicles that took us back to the hotel. For a little less price, I hired the next day a modern boat for 4 hours to do the same tour privately, with umbrella and snack, towels included. Which makes me think what I paid for the first one was a total scam.

## Review 739

Recogida puntual para llevarnos a Fumba. Pone Safari privado, pero es compartido con más personas. Fuimos a bucear a Menai bay y nos prepararon fruta, agua y bebidas. Luego nos llevaron a comer, buena organización y buena comida. Por la tarde nos llevaron a la laguna donde buceamos unos 10 min. Y nos llevaron de regreso a Fumba para coger el transporte y llevarnos de regreso al hotel.
Creo que para lo que es la excursión es un poco cara. Se pierde mucho tiempo en transporte.

## Review 740

Expensive for the experience, not well organized tbh, but ok. We felt that as we were only 2 people, they were in a hurry to finish and take a bigger group (more tips)

## Review 741

The drive from our hotel was interesting because we passed so many interesting villages, schools, and markets.  Excellent driver.  I really liked the park ranger who gave us the tour through the forest.  I could tell he is committed to conservation.  I recommend going in the morning.  The monkeys are more active then.  We did not opt for the night tour but it sounded interesting.

## Review 742

Unser Guide Rashidi war einfach der Hammer, der hat uns soviel erzählen und zeigen können, das war der Wahnsinn.

Der einzige kritische Punkt ist anzumerken, dass wenn man sich vom Hotel abholen lassen möchte zusätzliche Gebühren anfallen und man das vor der Buchung irgendwo anführen kann und nicht erst nach der Buchung irgendwo findet.

We had a great time on the Jozani forest tour. Our guide was extremely knowledgeable and shared so much information and history with us, and getting to see the monkeys at play, up close and personal was an amazing experience.

## Review 744

La visite du parc avec les singes et la mangrove qui est vraiment typique. Le guide francophone nous a bien expliqué et fait découvrir la culture locale pendant la visite et le trajet

## Review 745

To get to prison island is definitely an experience ~ waves are a bit rough. The island has full of history and wish our guide was knowledgeable about it. We relied on reading whatever we can find on display. Stone Town gave me chills due to historical circumstances and events that had occurred in that area. Again, we read everything which was great. We would have liked to receive info about other buildings or sites we saw nearby, when asked. We didn’t care about their current markets due to hard sale tactics. Spice town is overrated and good to see a couple of plants of 2 spices we usually use

## Review 746

This was such a fantastic tour.  Tour Guide was exceptional!!!! Extremely knowledgeable  about spice farm, Prison Island and Stone Town.  I learned more about Zanzibar in the one day than the entire time I was there.  He was very accommodating but kept us on schedule.  He was a wealth of knowledge!

## Review 747

Really enjoyable day out. The guide gave plenty of interesting information. Puck up at our hotel was on time and we had enough time for all three parts of the trip.

## Review 748

Learned a lot about history of island and its slave trade past. Learned about their government and culture. We are leaving the island knowing a lot more about its rich culture and importance in the world.

## Review 749

The guide (whose name I don''t quite remember) did a good job.  He showed up on time and was easy enough to understand. The order we did things was a bit different than shown but that didn''t matter one bit.  We started with the Spice Farm.  His knowledge of the spices and fruits seemed deep.  Him and one of the farmers took me around and had me guess which spice was which based on smell and taste so I recommend  studying up a bit beforehand if you want to do well.  There was a coconut tree climbing.and a fruit tasting.   They also gave you a chance to buy some of their wares but there was very little if any pressure to do so (especially compared to the pressure you''ll find in Stone Town).

Next we did the walking tour which I had done separately the day before so there wasn''t much new info but there was some.

After the walking tour we went to the beach and got a boat to Prison Island.  There we did the history of the island and looked at the turtles.   The island isn''tvery large and there really isn''t a ton to see so it doesn''t take super long. 

The only concern I had was that the guides knowledge for the walking tour and the island seemed like it was a bit lacking compared to the Spice Tour.

## Review 750

A wonderful experience. We have spent a great day starting with the spice plantation, then visiting the stone town center and then going to prison island to see the huge turtles they have as well as the rest of the island and facilities. Really a 10

## Review 751

We booked a tour of a spice farm, prison island and Stonetown. Our host was very knowledgable and accommodating. We needed to run errands at the local school and he helped us do that and showed us around each place on the tour, giving very detailed history and information. We were provided with so much education! Professional, safe, friendly and an expert in his role.

## Review 752

Fantastic tour with Sultan. We went to 6 different places on the island including nungwi beach, paje beach, the rock and stone town. Sultan is an incredibly knowledgeable guide and told he all about the history of Zanzibar as well as fun facts about the places we went. I learned so much and was entertained all day. Would highly recommend this tour especially to anyone who is short on time and wants a good overview of Zanzibar.

## Review 753

Excellent organization, I have always felt safe as a woman. An EXCELLENT experience. Thank you again.

## Review 754

The spice part of the experience was great, which is really what we were looking for. There was a description of the food cooking, and we didn’t realize that we were going to participate to the extent that we did and we also didn’t realize that the cooking part was almost twice as long as the spice part and there was no written instructions to take home so there’s no way we could ever duplicate it and it took a lot of time. I think your write up should be more descriptive. Basically it should be a cooking class with a spice tour. Not sure how you should mark this but we were disappointed in the cooking part. There was nothing wrong with it. It was well done by a lady who definitely knew what she was doing and she involved everyone, but that was not what we were really expecting.

## Review 755

My husband and I had a great experience with Splash Tours. The owner Abdi picked us up on time at the hotel and walked us to the car. Our driver then took us to the spice farm, where our guide Hassan provided a thorough and insightful tour, sharing the history of Zanzibar and of the various spices as well as their uses and benefits. He also answered my many questions and told us a lot about the many peoples and cultured that have shaped the archipelago and the country.
We smelled and picked different spices along the way. After a photo and musical break (including braided leaves turned into adornments for us and a singing tree climbing « butterfly man »), we took a cooking class with Habiba. The meal was DELICIOUS and we’re excited to make it again when we get home! My husband is vegetarian and they had vegetarian options for him, and fish & chicken options for me.

We also got to buy some local spices and speak at length with locals. We were very happy with the tour and recommend it to travelers who want an authentic experience with knowledgeable and friendly people. Asante sana!

## Review 756

This was a lovely experience! We had an on-time pickup. We enjoyed exploring, learning, smelling, and tasting the spices of Mtausa Spice Farm. And even more we enjoyed cooking as a group; our dishes turned out great!

My only suggestion is that the listing be updated to say “hotel pickup for a fee, if outside of Stonetown” there was a bit of confusion with if it was included or not in the beginning.

All said, we enjoyed the experience, and I would recommend.

## Review 757

amazing experience, simply amazing! the ride, the quad bikes, the villages, the farms and the only Zanzibar water dam was all an amazing experience. loved it all the way.

## Review 758

Great experience, market was really cool.  Mohammed was our guide.  Would recommend bringing cash in small bills.

## Review 759

We enjoyed his company very much and we learned a lot.   He listened to our questions and the answers were well-informed and honest.  The whole experience was very much tailored to our interests and speed.
We would love to return to Zanzibar to do other tours with him.

## Review 760

## Review 836

A wonderful opportunity to watch and participate in the preparation of a delicious Zanzibar meal made over a coal stove in a home. Halisi Africa Tours did a great job adjusting the schedule and preparations due to the weather (currently in the rainy season) so all ingredients were ready to go before the demonstration. I learned that Halisi means authentic, and that was the experience. Our chef (Whitee (sp)) was efficient, explained her steps, and I was given the opportunity to request the recipe. She used traditional cooking tools such as the seated coconut grater, and current kitchen appliances together to cook a delicious three-course meal over a coal fire. Most of the cooking was a demonstration, but I was given the opportunity to help with preparing some of the items that would be used in cooking. After all was prepared, we went into the living room for lunch, and everything was delicious. 

If you have any food allergies, please let them know as they take that into consideration, and honestly if you do attend this class, make sure you have a light breakfast so you can enjoy every last bit of this great meal, and enjoy the great fellowship.

## Review 889

If you''re visiting Zanzibar, do not miss the Stone Town city tour! It was the absolute highlight of our trip. The walking tour took me through the heart of the city’s history — from the Old Fort and House of Wonders , with historical stories told by Nassor that visually and vividly took me back in time.  Our guide, Nassor was incredibly knowledgeable and shared stories that gave life and meaning to every building and alley we walked through.

I was amazed by the blend of cultures in Stone Town — Arab, African, Indian, and European — and how beautifully it’s reflected in the architecture, food, and daily life. I also loved visiting the local markets and spice shops, where I was able to experience  Zanzibar’s spice heritage.

The pace was perfect, and we had plenty of time for questions, photos, and even shopping. The people of Stone Town were so friendly and welcoming. I left with a much deeper appreciation for Zanzibar’s rich past and vibrant present.

Highly recommend this tour — it’s informative, emotional, and absolutely unforgettable. If you are in luck you will have Nassor as your guide

Rakib is a great guide. He is personable and respectful, his English is excellent, his knowledge of Zanzibar history, art and architecture, food and culture is extensive, and he is a very effective communicator.  He is a very good and safe driver and he has a great sense of humor.   He asked what we already knew about Zanzibar and Stone City and what areas of the city we had already seen, and he took all of that into account as he toured us around.  He was quite accommodating in helping us with special requests (we needed a Post Office and we needed a particular football shirt), and he extended the tour almost an hour beyond the 3-hr posted tour duration.  Overall, an interesting and informative tour, and he is a very pleasant person to spend an afternoon with.

## Review 977

Para empezar, me aseguraron que sería en castellano, varias veces, fue el motivo de elegir este safari, y no fue así. Se subcontrata con otro proveedor, Beach Safari, y a pesar de haber 5 españoles, nos ponen en 2 coches y me aseguran que no hay ningún guia en Mikumi que hable español. Pero eato es anecdotico, es cosa de este proveedor. Lo i.portante es que, al menos en julio, no merece la pena. No hay animales, vas por caminos desde los que ves zonas de obras con camiones enormes trabajando, el lago donde nos llevan a ver hipopótamos y cocodrilos es artificial, nos damos cuenta enseguida, lo preguntamos al guia y nos lo confirma abiertamente, de verdad, me da pena, pero lo ham hecho para tener hipopotamos que enseñar, no se de donde los habran llevado. Como hay tan pocos animales, van quemando el pasto zona por zona, porque así en 2 semanas hay pasto verde y las pocas gacelas que hay se ve que se quedan. Pero esto hace que constantemente haya olor a humo. Parece todo preparado para los turistas. Nosotros hemos estado antes en Kenya, y esto es poco mas que ir al safari Madrid. Habíamos puesto muchísima ilusión en esto, íbamos con nuestro hijo de 6 años, le habiamos contado como fue nuestro viaje y de verdad que fue una gran, gran, gran decepción. No sabeis la pena que da ver como espera ver animales que no hay, y te pasas todo el.dia en el coche. No se si Selous es igual, pero Mikumi no merece la pena en absoluto. Es cierto que vimos elefantes, la.familia de leones que.parece que dan de comer para que se queden, 4 jirafas casualmente al lado de donde comes, 4 ñus, literal y de lejos, 4 cebras, y algunas gacelas, ocultes, monos y chimpancés, pero la realidad es que es una gran decepción.

## Review 998

Amazingly beautiful scenery, incredible to see so many different kinds of animals, very well organised with super friendly staff. One of my favourite days this year.

Pick-up was decent, well organised at the airport only having to wait 15 minutes before departure, flight was swift (30 min), the Land Rovers were in good condition and were comfy and we had a good lunch too.

We saw: elephants, hippos, bison, water-buffalo, giraffe, zebra, antelope (many kinds), pumbaa, baboons, crocodiles and more. Didn''t see lions or leopards though unfortunately (less common in rainy seasons in Selous).

Take note that this is a woodland safari rather than a savannah so there''s much more greenery, if you''re expecting wide-open plains, this isn''t that. But the benefits were that the scenery was probably more beautiful (the massive lakes and rivers), but you often couldn''t see as far as you would on a savannah. Also be aware during and just after the rainy seasons the animals are more dispersed as the animals'' food is more readily available so you''ll see more animals in the dry season when food is more concentrated. I went in December which is just after a rainy season and we still saw loads of animals though.

## Review 1010

Mohamed had a wealth of knowledge about the fascinating history of Zanzibar. The walk was very relaxed and he took time to stop whenever I expressed an interest in something. We watched a fish auction in the fish market and I learnt a lot about spices in the Spice market. The Slave Trade exhibition was harrowing but very informative. Mohamed was an excellent tour guide.

## Review 1011

By far the most interesting walking tour I have ever taken. Yes, the streets of Stone Town are a labyrinth of tiny streets and something different is around each corner. And the market is a must see in person experience. But our guide, Imran had an incredible wealth of knowledge and history, and presented all the conflicting and contrasting religions, politics and traditions that make Zanzibar so special. And he mapped out how it was from the past to current times. He was the best part of our visit to Stonetown, and we feel like he opened our eyes (and minds) to all the beautiful confluences that make Zanzibar what it is today.

## Review 1012

Mohammed was our guide. I was thrilled when it was him because I had read that he was excellent and he sure was. There was a little confusion (and lots of traffic) in the beginning trying to find the meeting place but when I was able to finally contact him via phone he was still waiting for us even though we were 45 minutes late; he had even gone 30 minutes earlier and was patiently waiting. I was very grateful to him for not giving up on us. He was extremely knowledgeable and thorough, explained even the smallest details of the town that we would have never paid attention to if we were alone. He was very pleasant and answered all of our questions and offered a tremendous amount of information and insight to the city and its history. Highly recommend the tour especially if you can get lucky and have Mohammed as your guide. Thank you Mohammed we will never forget you! Asante sana. Anna & Charlie.

## Review 1013

Imran gave us a fantastic tour of Stone Town!  He was prompt, knowledgeable very friendly and accommodating and gave us a great background and the history of what makes this town so special.   Our teenage children also enjoyed it!  Highly recommend!

## Review 1014

Imran is an exceptional guide. He is very knowledgable and delivers the history Stone town factually and with no bias.
We really enjoyed his company for the few hours.

## Review 1015

Booked a walking tour of Stone Town and it was easily one of the highlights of my Zanzibar trip. The guide Mohammed was incredibly knowledgeable and clearly passionate about the history of the area. We covered a lot of ground: three different mosques, the Catholic cathedral, the spice market and the former slave market, which is now a sobering but important museum. He gave rich historical context at each stop rather than just reciting facts, which made the whole experience feel personal. A fun highlight for music fans was the stop at the Freddie Mercury Museum, celebrating Zanzibar’s most famous son. Highly recommend this tour if you want to actually understand Stone Town rather than just wander through it.

## Review 1135

Iman (I think that’s how you spell his name) was our tour guide. He was very knowledgeable. It was raining so he came prepared with umbrellas which was nice. The tour was only 2 hours but it felt like even less than that because the time flew! We felt like we received a comprehensive history of Stone Town, Zanzibar, and the local culture. Its a great way to get immersed in the culture and learn about your surroundings.

## Review 1136

It was a very interesting tour and Imran was an excellent guide. I particularly enjoyed the covered market and the museum of the East African Slave Trade.

## Review 1137

Our tour guide Mohammed was excellent. I would highly recommend him. He was patient, knowledgeable and happy to answer our questions. He was prompt in meeting us at the Old Fort where our tour begin. As a coffee lover, he showed us the best coffee shop in Stonetown ''Zanzibar Coffee Shop''. Catering the tour to our needs. There was no pressure to buy when he took us to the spice market. Stone Town is a historic place and Mohammed brought it alive with his knowledge.

Very polite and knowledgeable guide, appreciate all the help. Even came and met us at our hotel lobby

## Review 1152

Great correspondence with Beach Safari Holidays from the go. Received a WhatsApp immediately with clear instructions. So pleased we booked this trip through Viator! So passionate about wildlife, as are we. He made the excursion, and we''re so thankful we had him as our guide! Also a special shout out to our driver Cholo! What a top bloke. He gave us so much local knowledge and insight to Zanzibar life. We''re so grateful to have spent this time with such fab people. Prison Island is o.k. Really enjoyed the boat trip over. There''s not a lot to do on the Island, but the beach is Amazing! You''ll get pestered in Stonetown for excursions, but really I would advise to go through Beach Safari Holidays. It was seamless, well organised and turned our excursions into a complete experience :)

## Review 1153

They picked us late we had to keep calling for them to come. 1st Driver was nice. The company agreed for an extra cost to take us to nakupenda sand bar. We paid ahead of time.So driver showed late again then we go to the boat ride..driver says take your luggage that would have a new driver after enjoying the sand bar. He then wanted to take our luggage to a random hotel storage himself and that someone else will pick it up before getting us after the boat ride. Then at the sandbar we were supposed to have a bbq seafood lunch and we never had it. Then they were late taking us back. We had to beg with only 1 and 1/2 hour left of our flight to take us back otherwise we will miss it. ## Review 1170

Adam was absolutely amazing! He shared so much about the local culture, food, language, and everyday life in such an engaging way. ## Review 1171

Eine tolle Tour mit einem sehr kompetenten Guide. Wir konnten einen Einblick in das Leben der einheimischen erhalten und durften viel über den aktuellen und vergangenen Lebensstil erfahren. Einer der besten Touren. Die Menschen waren sehr freundlich und haben sich Zeit für alle Fragen genommen und vieles ausführlich erklärt. Das Essen war hervorragend. Wir können es nur empfehlen.

## Review 1172

I am glad I got to see a bit of everyday life in Zanzibar! I especially enjoyed the witch doctor visit (her herbal stream actually did help my chronic headache). The home cooked lunch was nice too. Abdul the guide lives in the village and is super chill and answered all my questions. 

Being some cash to donate/tip people in the village for their time. Only the doctor at the hospital was at all pushy about it (he was very pushy actually), no one else even asked. IMO as a foreigner visiting here we are in a position of privilege and should redistribute some of that privilege where we can, especially when people are taking the time to show us their life.

## Review 1173

Nos vinieron a recoger al hotel y tanto Zahran el conductor como, Abdulkarm el guía, estuvieron atentos en casa momento explicando con detalle cada pregunta o duda q teníamos de la excursión y ayudándonos a pasar un día INOLVIDABLE, en una excursión q creo imprescindible en este viaje...

## Review 1174

Wonderful tour to understand the culture and lives of the  people. Achmec. Our driver was waiting for us at the port . Comfortable minivan with ac for the 90 min ride to village. Abdul was our guide and  lead us on a walk around the village . Visiting mommas who showed us how they make rope from coconut shells that are buried in the sea. We didn’t see a witch doctor but saw a momma herbalist who was also a midwife. Since it was  Ramadan no school. But Abdul gave us pencils to hand out to the children as we walked through. Their faces lit up! Final stop was the medical clinic . Since my wife and I are medical professionals we were very much interested in how they treated the locals. They do great work with limited resources. Lunch was at a local residence.  Highly encourage this tour if you want a real experience.

## Review 1175

If you are going to book this experience, check the itinerary with the guide before you set off. We ended up in the wrong location, not seeing any of the things mentioned. I would also suggest that you do not book at the weekends as apparently the witch doctor does not work. We found out the schools are on holiday for the Christmas period.

was an amazing experience! didn’t realize i had a private tour but my guide explained to me the entire history of Zanzibar + so many things about the people of Zanzibar. Didn’t expect it but learned so much on this tour! guide was extremely knowledgeable as well

## Review 1179

A fantastic experience in Mikumi with our guide Nuhu. He knew all about wildlife and birds. We learned a lot.  We saw the elephants, zebras, antelopes, Giraffes, monkeys, puumba, buffalo, hippon, lions and several beautiful birds. All transport worked great. We went by train to Morogoro which was nice, when we arrived we got food and later a visit to a Maasai village where we got lots of information about the Maasai lives. (Take the opportunity to buy handicrafts from the Maasai women) The second day we left early and came home late after a fantastic day in Mikumi National Park, lunch in the park. On the third day it was time to travel to Zanzibar by train and ferry. It was nice that the driver was waiting for us outside the ferry even though we were delayed and on to our accommodation in Jambiani.
